# Dasakanipāta (AN 10)

> **Nikāya:** Aṅguttaranikāya
> **Nipāta:** AN 10 (Dasakanipāta)
> **PTS Volume:** Aṅguttara Nikāya Vol. 5
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

---


<!-- PTS Page 001 -->
<a id="page-001"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 001]*

AṄGUTTARA-NIKĀYA. DASAKA-NIPĀTA.

<p align="center">Namo Tassa Bhagavato Arahato Sammāsambuddhassa.</p>


I.

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kimatthiyāni bhante kusalāni sīlāni kimānisaṃsānī'; ti? ‘Avippaṭisāratthāni kho[^001-1] Ānanda kusalāni sīlāni avippaṭisārānisaṃsānī'; ti[^001-2]. ‘Avippaṭisāro pana bhante kimatthiyo kimānisaṃso'; ti[^001-3]? ‘Avippaṭisāro kho Ānanda pāmujjattho pāmujjānisaṃso'; ti[^001-4]. ‘Pāmujjaṃ pana[^001-5] bhante kimatthiyaṃ kimānisaṃsan'; ti[^001-3]? ‘Pāmujjaṃ kho Ānanda pītatthaṃ pītānisaṃsan'; ti[^001-4]. ‘Pīti pana[^001-5] bhante kimatthiyā kimānisaṃsā'; ti[^001-3]? ‘Pīti kho Ānanda passaddhatthā passaddhānisaṃsā'; ti[^001-6]. ‘Passaddhi pana bhante kimatthiyā kimānisaṃsā'; ti[^001-3]? ‘Passaddhi kho Ānanda sukhatthā sukhānisaṃsā'; ti[^001-4]. ‘Sukhaṃ pana bhante kimatthiyaṃ kimānisaṃsan'; ti[^001-3]? ‘Sukhaṃ kho Ānanda samādhatthaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 002 -->
<a id="page-002"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 002]*

samādhānisaṃsan'; ti[^002-1]. ‘Samādhi pana[^002-2] bhante kimatthiyo kimānisaṃso'; ti[^002-3]? ‘Samādhi kho Ānanda yathābhūtañāṇadassanattho yathābhūtañāṇadassanānisaṃso'; ti[^002-1]. ‘Yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ pana[^002-4] bhante kimatthiyaṃ kimānisaṃsan'; ti[^002-3]? ‘Yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ kho Ānanda nibbidāvirāgatthaṃ nibbidāvirāgānisaṃsan'; ti[^002-1]. Nibbidāvirāgo pana bhante kimatthiyo kimānisaṃso'; ti[^002-3]? ‘Nibbidāvirāgo kho Ānanda vimuttiñāṇadassanattho vimuttiñāṇadassanānisaṃso'; ti[^002-1].

2. Iti kho Ānanda kusalāni sīlāni avippaṭisāratthāni avippaṭisārānisaṃsāni, avippaṭisāro pāmujjattho pāmujjānisaṃso, pāmujjaṃ pītatthaṃ pītānisaṃsaṃ, pīti passaddhatthā passaddhānisaṃsā, passaddhi sukhatthā sukhānisaṃsā, sukhaṃ samādhatthaṃ samādhānisaṃsaṃ, samādhi yathābhūtañāṇadassanattho yathābhūtañāṇadassanānisaṃso, yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ nibbidāvirāgatthaṃ nibbidāvirāgānisaṃsaṃ, nibbidāvirāgo vimuttiñāṇadassanattho vimuttiñāṇadassanānisaṃso. Iti kho Ānanda kusalāni sīlāni anupubbena aggāya[^002-5] parentī[^002-6] ti. II.

1. Sīlavato bhikkhave sīlasampannassa na[^002-7] cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘avippaṭisāro me uppajjatū'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ sīlavato sīlasampannassa[^002-8] avippaṭisāro uppajjati. Avippaṭisārissa[^002-9] bhikkhave na[^002-10] cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘pāmujjaṃ[^002-11] me uppajjatū'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ avippaṭisārissa[^002-12] pāmujjaṃ uppajjati[^002-13]. Pamuditassa bhikkhave na[^002-7] cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘pīti me uppajjatū'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ pamuditassa


<!-- PTS Page 003 -->
<a id="page-003"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 003]*

pīti[^003-1] uppajjati[^003-2]. Pītimanassa bhikkhave na cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘kāyo me passambhatū'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ pītimanassa kāyo passambhati. Passaddhakāyassa[^003-3] bhikkhave na cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘sukhaṃ vediyāmī'[^003-4] ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ passaddhakāyo[^003-5] sukhaṃ vediyati. Sukhino bhikkhave na cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘cittaṃ me samādhiyatū'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati. Samāhitassa bhikkhave na cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘yathābhūtaṃ jānāmi[^003-6] passāmī'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ samāhito yathābhūtaṃ jānāti[^003-6] passati. Yathābhūtaṃ bhikkhave jānato[^003-7] passato[^003-7] na cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘nibbindāmi[^003-8] virajjāmī'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ[^003-9] yathābhūtaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ nibbindati virajjati. Nibbindassa[^003-10] bhikkhave virattassa[^003-11] na cetanāya karaṇīyaṃ ‘vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ sacchikaromī'; ti. Dhammatā esā bhikkhave, yaṃ[^003-9] nibbindo[^003-12] viratto vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ sacchikaroti.

2. Iti kho bhikkhave nibbidāvirāgo[^003-13] vimuttiñāṇadassanattho[^003-14] vimuttiñāṇadassanānisaṃso, yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ nibbidāvirāgatthaṃ[^003-14] nibbidāvirāgānisaṃsaṃ, samādhi yathābhūtañāṇadassanattho[^003-15] yathābhūtañāṇadassanānisaṃso, sukhaṃ samādhatthaṃ samādhānisaṃsaṃ, passaddhi sukhatthā sukhānisaṃsā, pīti passaddhatthā[^003-16] passaddhānisaṃsā, pāmujjaṃ pītatthaṃ pītānisaṃsaṃ, avippaṭisāro pāmujjattho pāmujjānisaṃso, kusalāni sīlāni avippaṭisāratthāni avippaṭisārānisaṃsāni. Iti kho bhikkhave[^003-17] dhammā


<!-- PTS Page 004 -->
<a id="page-004"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 004]*

'va[^004-1] dhamme abhisandenti[^004-2], dhammā 'va dhamme paripūrenti apārā[^004-3] pāraṃ gamanāyā ti. III.

1. Dussīlassa bhikkhave sīlavipannassa[^004-4] hatupaniso[^004-5] hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre asati avippaṭisāravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti pāmujjaṃ, pāmujje asati pāmujjavipannassa hatupanisā hoti pīti, pītiyā asati pītivipannassa hatupanisā hoti passaddhi, passaddhiyā asati passaddhivipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti sukhaṃ, sukhe asati sukhavipannassa hatupaniso hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipannassa hatupaniso hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge asati nibbidāvirāgavipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho sākhāpalāsavipanno, tassa papaṭikā[^004-6] pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave[^004-7] dussīlassa sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre asati avippaṭisāravipannassa[^004-8] hatupanisaṃ hoti[^004-9] . . . pe[^004-10] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ[^004-11].

2. Sīlavato bhikkhave sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre sati avippaṭisārasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti pāmujjaṃ, pāmujje sati pāmujjasampannassa upanisasampannā hoti pīti, pītiyā sati pītisampannassa upanisasampannā hoti passaddhi, passaddhiyā sati passaddhisampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti sukhaṃ, sukhe sati sukhasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti sammā-


<!-- PTS Page 005 -->
<a id="page-005"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 005]*

samādhi, sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampannassa upanisampanno hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge sati nibbidāvirāgasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho sākhāpalāsasampanno, tassa papaṭikā pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave sīlavato sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre sati avippaṭisārasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti[^005-1] . . . pe[^005-2] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanan ti. IV.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Sāriputto bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Dussīlassa āvuso sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre asati avippaṭisāravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti . . . pe[^005-3] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi āvuso rukkho sākhāpalāsavipanno, tassa papaṭikā pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho āvuso dussīlassa sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre asati avippaṭisāravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti[^005-1] . . . pe[^005-4] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ.

2. Sīlavato āvuso sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre sati avippaṭisārasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti . . . pe[^005-5] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi āvuso rukkho sākhāpalāsasampanno, tassa papaṭikā pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho āvuso sīlavato sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre sati avippaṭisārasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti[^005-1] . . . pe[^005-6] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanan ti.


<!-- PTS Page 006 -->
<a id="page-006"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 006]*

V.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Ānando bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Dussīlassa āvuso sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre asati avippaṭisāravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti pāmujjaṃ, pāmujje asati pāmujjavipannassa hatupanisā hoti pīti, pītiyā asati pītivipannassa hatupanisā hoti passaddhi, passaddhiyā asati passaddhivipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti sukhaṃ, sukhe asati sukhavipannassa hatupaniso hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipannassa hatupaniso hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge asati nibbidāvirāgavipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi āvuso rukkho sākhāpalāsavipanno, tassa papaṭikā pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho āvuso dussīlassa sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre asati avippaṭisāravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti[^006-1] . . . pe[^006-2] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ.

2. Sīlavato āvuso sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre sati avippaṭisārasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti pāmujjaṃ, pāmujje sati[^006-3] pāmujjasampannassa upanisasampannā hoti pīti, pītiyā sati pītisampannassa upanisasampannā hoti passaddhi, passaddhiyā sati passaddhisampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti sukhaṃ, sukhe sati sukhasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge sati nibbidāvirāgasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi āvuso rukkho


<!-- PTS Page 007 -->
<a id="page-007"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 007]*

sākhāpalāsasampanno, tassa papaṭikā pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho āvuso sīlavato sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti avippaṭisāro, avippaṭisāre sati avippaṭisārasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti[^007-1] . . . pe[^007-2] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanan ti. VI.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā[^007-3] Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘siyā nu kho bhante bhikkhuno tathārūpo samādhipaṭilābho, yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ[^007-4] paṭhavisaññī assa, na āpasmiṃ āposaññī assa, na tejasmiṃ tejosaññī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyosaññī assa, na ākāsānañcāyatane[^007-5] ākāsānañcāyatanasaññī assa, na viññāṇañcāyatane viññāṇañcāyatanasaññī assa, na ākiñcaññāyatane ākiñcaññāyatanasaññī assa, na nevasaññānāsaññāyatane nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññī assa, na idhaloke idhalokasaññī assa, na paraloke paralokasaññī assa, saññī[^007-6] ca pana assā'; ti? ‘Siyā[^007-7] Ānanda bhikkhuno tathārūpo samādhipaṭilābho, yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ paṭhavisaññī assa, na āpasmiṃ āposaññī assa, na tejasmiṃ tejosaññī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyosaññī assa, na ākāsānañcāyatane ākāsānañcāyatanasaññī assa, na viññāṇañcāyatane[^007-7] viññāṇañcāyatanasaññī assa, na ākiñcaññāyatane ākiñcaññāyatanasaññī assa, na nevasaññānāsaññāyatane nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññī assa, na idhaloke idhalokasaññī assa, na paraloke paralokasaññī assa, saññī ca pana assā'; ti.

2. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana bhante siyā bhikkhuno tathārūpo samādhipaṭilābho, yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ paṭhavisaññī assa, na[^007-8] āpasmiṃ āposaññī assa, na[^007-8] tejasmiṃ tejosaññī


<!-- PTS Page 008 -->
<a id="page-008"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 008]*

assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyosaññī assa, na ākāsānañcāyatane ākāsānañcāyatanasaññī assa, na viññāṇañcāyatane viññāṇañcāyatanasaññī assa, na ākiñcaññāyatane ākiñcaññāyatanasaññī assa, na nevasaññānāsaññāyatane nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññī assa, na[^008-1] idhaloke idhalokasaññī assa, na[^008-1] paraloke paralokasaññī assa, saññī[^008-2] ca pana assā'; ti? ‘Idh'; Ānanda[^008-3] bhikkhu evaṃsaññī[^008-4] hoti: etaṃ santaṃ, etaṃ paṇītaṃ, yad idaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānan ti. Evaṃ kho Ānanda siyā bhikkhuno yathārūpo samādhipaṭilābho, yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ paṭhavisaññī assa, na āpasmiṃ āposaññī assa, na tejasmiṃ tejosaññī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyosaññī assa, na ākāsānañcāyatane ākāsānañcāyatanasaññī assa, na viññāṇañcāyatane viññāṇañcāyatanasaññī assa, na ākiñcaññāyatane ākiñcaññāyatanasaññī assa, na nevasaññānāsaññāyatane nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññī assa, na idhaloke idhalokasaññī assa, na paraloke paralokasaññī assa, saññī ca pana assā'; ti. VII.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yenāyasmā Sāriputto ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Sāriputtena saddhiṃ sammodi[^008-5], sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^008-6] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ etad avoca ‘siyā nu kho āvuso Sāriputta bhikkhuno tathārūpo samādhipaṭilābho, yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ paṭhavisaññī assa, na āpasmiṃ āposaññī assa, na tejasmiṃ tejosaññī assa, na vāyasmiṃ vāyosaññī assa, na ākāsānañcāyatane ākāsānañcāyatana-


<!-- PTS Page 009 -->
<a id="page-009"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 009]*

saññī assa, na viññāṇañcāyatane viññāṇañcāyatanasaññī assa, na ākiñcaññāyatane ākiñcaññāyatanasaññī assa, na nevasaññānāsaññāyatane nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññī assa, na idhaloke idhalokasaññī assa, na paraloke paralokasaññī assa, saññī[^009-1] ca pana assā'; ti? ‘Siyā āvuso Ānanda bhikkhuno tathārūpo samādhipaṭilābho, yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ paṭhavisaññī assa . . . pe[^009-2] . . . na paraloke paralokasaññī assa, saññī ca pana assā'; ti.

2. ‘Yathākathaṃ panāvuso Sāriputta siyā bhikkhuno tathārūpo samādhipaṭilābho, yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ paṭhavisaññī assa . . . pe[^009-3] . . . na[^009-4] paraloke paralokasaññī assa, saññī ca pana assā'; ti? ‘Ekam idāhaṃ āvuso Ānanda samayaṃ idh'; eva Sāvatthiyaṃ viharāmi Andhavanasmiṃ, tatthāhaṃ[^009-5] tathārūpaṃ[^009-6] samādhiṃ samāpajjiṃ[^009-7], yathā neva paṭhaviyaṃ paṭhavisaññī ahosiṃ[^009-8], na āpasmiṃ āposaññī ahosiṃ, na tejasmiṃ tejosaññī ahosiṃ, na vāyasmiṃ vāyosaññī ahosiṃ, na ākāsānañcāyatane ākāsānañcāyatanasaññī ahosiṃ, na viññāṇañcāyatane viññāṇañcāyatanasaññī ahosiṃ, na ākiñcaññāyatane ākiñcaññāyatanasaññī ahosiṃ, na nevasaññānāsaññāyatane nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññī ahosiṃ, na idhaloke idhalokasaññī ahosiṃ, na paraloke paralokasaññī ahosiṃ, saññī ca pana ahosin'; ti.

3. ‘Kiṃsaññī panāyasmā[^009-9] Sāriputto tasmiṃ samaye ahosī'; ti? ‘Bhavanirodho nibbānaṃ, bhavanirodho nibbānan ti kho me[^009-10] āvuso aññā 'va[^009-11] saññā uppajjati, aññā 'va[^009-12] saññā nirujjhati. Seyyathā pi āvuso sakalikaggissa jhāyamānassa[^009-13] aññā 'va[^009-11] acci[^009-14] uppajjati, aññā 'va[^009-15] acci[^009-14] nirujjhati, evam eva kho me[^009-16] āvuso bhavanirodho[^009-17] nibbānaṃ[^009-17], bhavanirodho nibbānan ti aññā 'va[^009-12] saññā


<!-- PTS Page 010 -->
<a id="page-010"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 010]*

uppajjati, aññā 'va[^010-1] saññā nirujjhati, bhavanirodho nibbānaṃ[^010-2]-saññī ca panāhaṃ āvuso tasmiṃ samaye ahosin'; ti. VIII.

1. Saddho ca[^010-3] bhikkhave bhikkhu hoti no ca sīlavā. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti. Tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ ‘kintāhaṃ saddho ca[^010-4] assaṃ[^010-5] sīlavā cā'; ti. Yato ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho ca hoti[^010-6] sīlavā ca, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hoti.

2. ##### *[PTS Page 015]*

XI.

1. Pañcaṅgasamannāgato bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgataṃ senāsanaṃ sevamāno bhajamāno na cirass' eva āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyya. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgato hoti?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho hoti saddahati Tathāgatassa bodhiṃ ‘iti pi so Bhagavā[^015-1] arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti, appābādho hoti appātaṅko samavepākiniyā[^015-2] gahaṇiyā samannāgato nātisītāya nāccuṇhāya[^015-3] majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya, asaṭho[^015-4] hoti amāyāvī yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikattā Satthari vā viññūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu, āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu, paññavā hoti udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgato hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave senāsanaṃ pañcaṅgasamannāgataṃ hoti?

3. Idha bhikkhave senāsanaṃ nātidūraṃ hoti nāccāsannaṃ[^015-3] gamanāgamanasampannaṃ, divā appakiṇṇaṃ[^015-5] rattiṃ appasaddaṃ appanigghosaṃ, appaḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassaṃ[^015-6], tasmiṃ kho pana senāsane viharantassa appakasiren'[^015-7] eva[^015-7] uppajjanti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārā, tasmiṃ kho pana senāsane therā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā


<!-- PTS Page 016 -->
<a id="page-016"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 016]*

dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, te kālena kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati ‘idaṃ[^016-1] bhante kathaṃ, imassa ko attho'; ti? Tassa te āyasmanto avivaṭañ c'; eva vivaranti anuttānikatañ[^016-2] ca uttānikaronte[^016-2] anekavihitesu ca kaṅkhāṭṭhāniyesu dhammesu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinodenti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave senāsanaṃ pañcaṅgasamannāgataṃ hoti. Pañcaṅgasamannāgato kho[^016-3] bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgataṃ senāsanaṃ sevamāno bhajamāno na cirass' eva āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^016-4] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā[^016-5] ti. XII.

1. Pañcaṅgavippahīno bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgato imasmiṃ dhammavinaye ‘kevalī[^016-6] vusitavā uttamapuriso'; ti vuccati. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgavippahīno hoti?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno kāmacchando pahīno hoti, vyāpādo pahīno hoti, thīnamiddhaṃ pahīnaṃ hoti, uddhaccakukkuccaṃ[^016-7] pahīnaṃ[^016-7] hoti[^016-7], vicikicchā pahīnā hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgavippahīno hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgato hoti?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu asekhena sīlakkhandhena samannāgato hoti, asekhena samādhikkhandhena samannāgato hoti, asekhena paññākkhandhena samannāgato hoti, asekhena vimuttikkhandhena samannāgato hoti, asekhena vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhena samannāgato hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgato hoti. Pañcaṅgavippahīno kho bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgasamannāgato imasmiṃ dhammavinaye ‘kevalī[^016-6] vusitavā uttamapuriso'[^016-8] ti vuccatī ti. Kāmacchando ca[^016-9] vyāpādo thīnamiddhañ ca bhikkhuno uddhaccaṃ vicikicchā ca[^016-10] sabbaso 'va[^016-11] na vijjati,


<!-- PTS Page 017 -->
<a id="page-017"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 017]*

asekhena ca sīlena asekhena samādhinā vimuttiyā ca sampanno ñāṇena ca tathāvidho: sa ve[^017-1] pañcaṅgasampanno pañca[^017-2] aṅge[^017-2] vivajjayaṃ[^017-3] imasmiṃ[^017-4] dhammavinaye kevalī[^017-5] iti vuccatī ti. XIII.

1. Dasa yimāni[^017-6] bhikkhave saṃyojanāni. Katamāni dasa?

2. Pañc'; orambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni pañc'; uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni. Katamāni pañc'; orambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni?

3. Sakkāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāso kāmacchando vyāpādo. Imāni pañc'; orambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni. Katamāni pañc'; uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni?

4. Rūparāgo arūparāgo māno uddhaccaṃ avijjā. Imāni pañc'; uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni. Imāni kho bhikkhave dasa saṃyojanānī ti. XIV.

1. Yassa kassaci[^017-7] bhikkhave bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā pañca cetokhilā appahīnā pañca cetaso vinibandhā[^017-8] asamucchinnā, tassa yā ratti[^017-9] vā divaso vā āgacchati, hāni yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu no vuddhi[^017-10]. Katamassa pañca cetokhilā appahīnā honti?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati. Yo[^017-11] so bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya


<!-- PTS Page 018 -->
<a id="page-018"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 018]*

sātaccāya padhānāya, evam assāyaṃ paṭhamo cetokhilo[^018-1] appahīno hoti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhamme[^018-2] kaṅkhati . . . pe[^018-3] . . . saṅghe kaṅkhati . . . sikkhāya[^018-4] kaṅkhati[^018-4] . . . sabrahmacārīsu kupito hoti anattamano āhatacitto khilajāto. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupito hoti anattamano āhatacitto khilajāto, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, evam assāyaṃ pañcamo cetokhilo appahīno hoti. Imassa pañca cetokhilā appahīnā honti. Katamassa pañca cetaso vinibandhā[^018-5] asamucchinnā honti?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu avītarāgo hoti avigatacchando avigatapemo avigatapipāso avigatapariḷāho avigatataṇho. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu avītarāgo hoti avigatacchando avigatapemo avigatapipāso avigatapariḷāho avigatataṇho, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, evam assāyaṃ paṭhamo cetaso vinibandho asamucchinno hoti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye avītarāgo hoti . . . pe[^018-6] . . . rūpe avītarāgo hoti, yāvadatthaṃ[^018-7] udarāvadehakaṃ bhuñjitvā seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ[^018-8] middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharati, aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya[^018-9] brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya[^018-10] brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati


<!-- PTS Page 019 -->
<a id="page-019"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 019]*

ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya[^019-1] padhānāya[^019-1], evam assāyaṃ pañcamo cetaso vinibandho asamucchinno hoti. Imassa pañca cetaso vinibandhā asamucchinnā honti. Yassa kassaci bhikkhave bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā ime pañca cetokhilā appahīnā ime[^019-2] pañca cetaso vinibandhā asamucchinnā, tassa yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, hāni yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu no vuddhi. Seyyathā[^019-3] pi bhikkhave kāḷapakkhe candassa yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati hāyat'; eva vaṇṇena hāyati maṇḍalena hāyati ābhāya hāyati ārohapariṇāhena, evam eva kho bhikkhave yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā ime pañca cetokhilā appahīnā ime pañca cetaso vinibandhā asamucchinnā, tassa yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, hāni yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu no vuddhi.

6. Yassa kassaci bhikkhave bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā pañca cetokhilā pahīnā pañca cetaso vinibandhā samucchinnā, tassa yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, vuddhi[^019-4] yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu no parihāni. Katamassa pañca cetokhilā pahīnā honti?

7. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuccati sampasīdati[^019-5]. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuccati sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ namati[^019-6] ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ namati[^019-6] ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, evam assāyaṃ paṭhamo cetokhilo pahīno[^019-7] hoti.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhamme na kaṅkhati . . . pe[^019-8] . . . saṅghe na kaṅkhati . . . sikhāya na


<!-- PTS Page 020 -->
<a id="page-020"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 020]*

kaṅkhati . . . sabrahmacārīsu na kupito hoti attamano na[^020-1] āhatacitto na[^020-1] khilajāto[^020-2]. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu na kupito hoti . . . pe[^020-3] . . . evam assāyaṃ pañcamo cetokhilo pahīno hoti. Imassa pañca cetokhilā pahīnā honti. Katamassa pañca cetaso vinibandhā susamucchinnā honti?

9. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu vītarāgo hoti vigatacchando vigatapemo vigatapipāso vigatapariḷāho vigatataṇho. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu vītarāgo hoti vigatacchando vigatapemo vigatapipāso vigatapariḷāho vigatataṇho, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa[^020-4] cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, evam assāyaṃ paṭhamo cetaso vinibandho susamucchinno hoti.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye[^020-5] vītarāgo hoti . . . pe[^020-6] . . . rūpe vītarāgo hoti[^020-7], na yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadehakaṃ bhuñjitvā seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ[^020-8] middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharati, na aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya[^020-9] brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu na aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya[^020-10] brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti, tassa cittaṃ namati[^020-11] ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātaccāya padhānāya, evam assāyaṃ pañcamo cetaso vinibandho susamucchinno hoti. Imassa pañca cetaso vinibandhā susamucchinnā honti. Yassa kassaci bhikkhave bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā ime pañca cetokhilā pahīnā ime[^020-12] pañca cetaso vinibandhā


<!-- PTS Page 021 -->
<a id="page-021"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 021]*

susamucchinnā, tassa yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, vuddhi[^021-1] yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu no parihāni. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave juṇhapakkhe candassa yā ratti[^021-2] vā divaso vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhat'; eva vaṇṇena vaḍḍhati maṇḍalena vaḍḍhati ābhāya vaḍḍhati ārohapariṇāhena, evam eva kho bhikkhave yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā ime pañca cetokhilā pahīnā ime[^021-3] pañca cetaso vinibandhā susamucchinnā, tassa yā ratti vā divaso vā āgacchati, vuddhi yeva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu no parihānī ti. XV.

1. Yāvatā bhikkhave sattā apadā[^021-4] vā dipadā[^021-5] vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpino vā[^021-6] arūpino vā[^021-6] saññino vā asaññino vā nevasaññināsaññino vā, Tathāgato tesaṃ aggam akkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddho: evam eva kho bhikkhave ye keci kusalā dhammā, sabbe te appamādamūlakā appamādasamosaraṇā, appamādo tesaṃ dhammānaṃ[^021-7] aggam akkhāyati.

2. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ pāṇānaṃ[^021-6] padajātāni[^021-8], sabbāni tāni hatthipade[^021-9] samodhānaṃ[^021-10] gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ[^021-11] tesaṃ aggam akkhāyati, yad[^021-6] idaṃ[^021-6] mahantattena[^021-6]: evam eva kho bhikkhave ye keci kusalā dhammā, sabbe te appamādamūlakā appamādasamosaraṇā, appamādo tesaṃ dhammānaṃ[^021-7] aggam akkhāyati. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gopānasiyo, sabbā tā kūṭaṅgamā kūṭaninnā kūṭasamosaraṇā, kūṭaṃ tāsaṃ aggam akkhāyati: evam eva kho bhikkhave ye keci kusalā dhammā, sabbe te appamādamūlakā appamādasamosaraṇā, appamādo tesaṃ dhammānaṃ[^021-7] aggam akkhāyati.

##### *[PTS Page 022]*

Seyyathā pi bhikkhave ye keci mūlagandhā, kāḷānusāriyaṃ[^022-1] tesaṃ aggam akkhāyati: evam eva kho bhikkhave ye[^022-2] keci[^022-2] kusalā[^022-2] . . . pe[^022-3] . . . Seyyathā pi bhikkhave ye keci sāragandhā, lohitacandanaṃ tesaṃ aggam akkhāyati: evam eva kho bhikkhave . . . pe[^022-3] . . . Seyyathā pi bhikkhave yā[^022-7] kāci[^022-7] tārakarūpānaṃ pabhā, sabbā tā candappabhāya[^022-8] kalaṃ nāgghanti[^022-9] soḷasiṃ[^022-10], candappabhā tāsaṃ aggam akkhāyati: evam eva bhikkhave . . . pe[^022-3] . . . Seyyathā pi bhikkhave saradasamaye viddhe[^022-11] vigatavalāhake deve ādicco nabhaṃ abbhussukkamāno[^022-12] sabbaṃ ākāsagataṃ tamagataṃ[^022-13] abhivihacca bhāsate ca tapate ca virocati[^022-14] ca[^022-15]: evam eva kho bhikkhave . . . pe[^022-3] . . . Seyyathā pi bhikkhave yā kāci mahānadiyo, seyyathīdaṃ Gaṅgā Yamunā Aciravatī Sarabhū Mahī, sabbā tā samuddaṅgamā[^022-16] samuddaninnā samuddapoṇā samuddapabbhārā, mahāsamuddo tāsaṃ[^022-17] aggam akkhāyati: evam eva kho bhikkhave ye keci kusalā dhammā, sabbe te appamādamūlakā appamādasamosaraṇā, appamādo tesaṃ dhammānaṃ[^022-18] aggam akkhāyatī ti.

##### *[PTS Page 023]*

XVI.

1. Dasa yime[^023-1] bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame dasa?

2. Tathāgato arahaṃ sammāsambuddho, paccekasambuddho[^023-2], ubhatobhāgavimutto. paññāvimutto[^023-3], kāyasakkhi, diṭṭhippatto, saddhāvimutto, dhammānusārī[^023-4], saddhānusārī, gotrabhū. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa puggalā āhuneyyā . . . pe[^023-5] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. XVII.

1. Sanāthā bhikkhave viharatha, mā anāthā. Dukkhaṃ bhikkhave anātho viharati. Dasa yime[^023-1] bhikkhave nāthakaraṇā dhammā. Katame dasa?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu[^023-6] vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu sīlavā hoti . . . pe[^023-7] . . . samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^023-8] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^023-9] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu bahussuto hoti . . . pe[^023-7] . . . diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto hoti

<a id="page-024"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 024]*

kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu suvaco[^024-1] hoti sovacassakaraṇehi[^024-2] dhammehi samannāgato khamo padakkhiṇaggāhī[^024-3] anusāsaniṃ[^024-4]. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu suvaco[^024-5] hoti[^024-6] sovacassakaraṇehi dhammehi samannāgato khamo padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu, yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ[^024-10] . . . pe[^024-11] . . . alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammakāmo hoti piyasamudāhāro[^024-12] abhidhamme abhivinaye uḷārapāmujjo[^024-13]. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammakāmo hoti piyasamudāhāro abhidhamme abhivinaye uḷārapāmujjo[^024-13]: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ[^024-14] dhammānaṃ[^024-14] pahānāya[^024-14], kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo viharati[^024-15] akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ[^024-16] pahānāya[^024-17] kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu santuṭṭho hoti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu santuṭṭho hoti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena: ayam pi dhammo nāthakaraṇo.

1. Dasa yime[^029-5] bhikkhave ariyavāsā[^029-6], ye[^029-7] ariyā[^029-7] āvasiṃsu[^029-8] vā āvasanti[^029-9] vā āvasissanti[^029-9] vā. Katame dasa?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgavippahīno hoti, chaḷaṅgasamannāgato, ekārakkho[^029-10], caturāpasseno[^029-11], panuṇṇapaccekasacco[^029-12], samavayasaṭṭhesano[^029-13], anāvilasaṅkappo, passaddhakāyasaṅkhāro[^029-14], suvimuttacitto, suvimuttapañño. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa ariyavāsā[^029-15], ye[^029-16] ariyā āvasiṃsu[^029-17] vā āvasanti[^029-18] vā āvasissanti[^029-19] vā ti. XX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kurūsu viharati Kammāsa-


<!-- PTS Page 030 -->
<a id="page-030"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 030]*

dhammaṃ[^030-1] nāma Kurūnaṃ nigamo. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi . . .[^030-2] Bhagavā[^030-3] etad[^030-3] avoca[^030-3]:--

2. Dasa yime[^030-4] bhikkhave ariyavāsā, ye[^030-5] ariyā āvasiṃsu[^030-6] vā āvasanti[^030-7] vā āvasissanti[^030-8] vā. Katame dasa?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgavippāhīno hoti, chaḷaṅgasamannāgato, ekārakkho, caturāpasseno[^030-8], panuṇṇapaccekasacco[^030-10], samavayasaṭṭhesano, anāvilasaṅkappo, passaddhakāyasaṅkhāro[^030-11], suvimuttacitto, suvimuttapañño. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgavippahīno hoti?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno kāmacchando pahīno hoti, vyāpādo pahīno hoti, thīnamiddhaṃ pahīnaṃ hoti, uddhaccakukkaccaṃ pahīnaṃ hoti, vicikicchā pahīnā hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcaṅgavippahīno hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasamannāgato hoti?

5. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā neva sumano hoti na dummano upekhako[^030-12] viharati sato sampajāno, sotena saddaṃ sutvā . . . pe[^030-13] . . . ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā, jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā, kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā, manasā dhammaṃ viññāya neva sumano hoti na dummano upekhako viharati sato sampajāno. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasamannāgato hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu ekārakkho hoti?

6. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu satārakkhena cetasā samannāgato hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu ekārakkho hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu caturāpasseno hoti?

7. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu saṅkhāy'; ekaṃ paṭisevati, saṅkhāy'; ekaṃ adhivāseti, saṅkhāy'; ekaṃ parivajjeti, saṅkhāy'; ekaṃ vinodeti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu caturāpasseno[^030-14] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 031 -->
<a id="page-031"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 031]*

Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu panuṇṇapaccekasacco hoti?

8. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno, yāni tāni puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ puthupaccekasaccāni, seyyathīdaṃ sassato loko ti[^031-1] vā[^031-1] asassato[^031-2] loko ti[^031-1] vā antavā loko ti vā anantavā loko ti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīran ti vā, aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīran ti vā, hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā, na[^031-3] hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā, hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā, neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā, sabbāni[^031-4] tāni nuṇṇāni[^031-5] honti panuṇṇāni[^031-6] cattāni vantāni muttāni pahīnāni paṭinissaṭṭhāni. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu panuṇṇapaccekasacco hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhesano hoti?

9. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno kāmesanā pahīnā hoti[^031-7], bhavesanā pahīnā hoti[^031-8], brahmacariyesanā paṭippassaddhā. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhesano hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappo hoti?

10. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno kāmasaṅkappo pahīno hoti, vyāpādasaṅkappo pahīno hoti, vihiṃsāsaṅkappo pahīno hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappo hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhāro hoti?

11. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubb'; eva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^031-9] adukkhamasukhaṃ upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ[^031-10] jhānaṃ[^031-10] upasampajja viharati. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhāro hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu. suvimuttacitto[^031-11] hoti?

12. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno rāgā[^031-12] cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hoti, dosā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hoti, mohā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu suvimuttacitto[^031-11] hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu suvimuttapañño hoti?


<!-- PTS Page 032 -->
<a id="page-032"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 032]*

13. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ‘rāgo me pahīno ucchinnamūlo tālāvatthukato anabhāvaṃ kato āyatiṃ anuppādadhammo'; ti pajānāti, ‘doso me pahīno . . . pe[^032-1] . . . moho me pahīno ucchinnamūlo tālāvatthukato anabhāvaṃ kato āyatiṃ anuppādadhammo'; ti pajānāti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu suvimuttapañño hoti.

14. Ye hi keci bhikkhave[^032-2] atītam addhānaṃ ariyā ariyavāse[^032-3] āvasiṃsu[^032-4], sabbe te im'; eva dasa ariyavāse[^032-5] āvasiṃsu[^032-4]. Ye hi keci bhikkhave[^032-2] anāgatam addhānaṃ ariyā ariyavāse[^032-5] āvasissanti[^032-6], sabbe te im'; eva[^032-7] dasa ariyavāse[^032-8] āvasissanti[^032-6]. Ye hi keci bhikkhave[^032-9] etarahi ariyā ariyavāse[^032-8] āvasanti[^032-6], sabbe te im'; eva dasa ariyavāse[^032-3] āvasanti[^032-6]. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa ariyavāsā, ye[^032-10] ariyā āvasiṃsu[^032-4] vā āvasanti[^032-6] vā āvasissanti[^032-6] vā ti[^032-11]. Nāthavaggo[^032-12] dutiyo. Tatr'[^032-13] uddānaṃ[^032-14]: Senāsanā ca aṅgāni[^032-15] saṃyojanakhilena[^032-16] ca Appamādo āhuneyyo dve nāthā dve ariyavāsena[^032-17] cā ti. XXI.

1. Sīho bhikkhave migarājā sāyaṇhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhamati, āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhati, vijambhitvā samantā catuddisā anuviloketi, samantā[^032-18] catuddisā[^032-18]


<!-- PTS Page 033 -->
<a id="page-033"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 033]*

anuviloketvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadati[^033-1], tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gocarāya pakkamati. Taṃ kissa hetu? ‘Māhaṃ khuddake pāṇe visamagate saṃghātaṃ āpādesin'[^033-2] ti. Sīho ti kho bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa. Yaṃ kho bhikkhave Tathāgato parisāya dhammaṃ deseti, idam assa hoti sīhanādasmiṃ. Dasa yimāni[^033-3] bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalāni, yehi balehi samannāgato Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti. Katamāni dasa?

2. Idha bhikkhave Tathāgato ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānaso hetuso vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato sabbatthagāminipaṭipadaṃ[^033-4] yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato sabbatthagāminipaṭipadaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato anekadhātu[^033-5]-


<!-- PTS Page 034 -->
<a id="page-034"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 034]*

nānādhātu[^034-1]-lokaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato anekadhātu[^034-1]-nānādhātu[^034-1]-lokaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ[^034-2] balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikataṃ[^034-3] yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikataṃ[^034-4] yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ[^034-2] balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparopariyattaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparopariyattaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ[^034-2] balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato jhānavimokhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ[^034-5] saṃkilesaṃ vodānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam pi bhikkhave[^034-6] Tathāgato jhānavimokhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilesaṃ vodānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ[^034-2] balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ‘ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo tisso pi jātiyo catasso pi jātiyo pañca[^034-7] pi jātiyo dasa pi jātiyo vīsam[^034-8] pi jātiyo tiṃsam pi jātiyo cattārisam[^034-9]


<!-- PTS Page 035 -->
<a id="page-035"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 035]*

pi jātiyo paññāsam pi jātiyo jātisatam pi jātisahassam pi jātisatasahassam[^035-1] pi[^035-1] aneke pi saṃvaṭṭakappe aneke pi vivaṭṭakappe aneke pi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe amutrāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto amutra udapādiṃ[^035-2], tatrāpāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto idh'; upapanno'[^035-3] ti. Iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^035-4] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^035-5] satte passati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^035-6] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānāti ‘ime vata bhonto sattā kāyaduccaritena samannāgatā vacīduccaritena[^035-7] samannāgatā manoduccaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā[^035-8] micchāditthikammasamādānā[^035-9], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapannā[^035-10]; ime vā pana bhonto sattā kāyasucaritena samannāgatā vacīsucaritena[^035-7] samannāgatā manosucaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā[^035-11], te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapannā'[^035-10] ti. Iti dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena satte passati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^035-6] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānāti.


<!-- PTS Page 036 -->
<a id="page-036"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 036]*

Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena . . . pe[^036-1] . . . yathākammūpage satte pajānāti, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

11. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ[^036-2] diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Yam pi bhikkhave Tathāgato āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati, idam pi bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti. Imāni kho bhikkhave Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalāni, yehi balehi samannāgato Tathāgato[^036-2] āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavattetī ti. XXII.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Ye te Ānanda dhammā tesaṃ tesaṃ adhimuttipadānaṃ[^036-3] abhiññā sacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti, visārado ahaṃ Ānanda tattha[^036-4] paṭijānāmi tesaṃ tesaṃ[^036-5] tathā tathā[^036-5] dhammaṃ desetuṃ, yathā yathā[^036-5] paṭipanno santaṃ vā ‘atthī'; ti ñassati, asantaṃ vā ‘natthī'; ti ñassati, hīnaṃ vā ‘hīnan'; ti ñassati, paṇītaṃ vā ‘paṇītan'; ti ñassati, sa-uttaraṃ[^036-6] vā ‘sa-uttaran'[^036-6] ti ñassati, anuttaraṃ vā ‘anuttaran'; ti ñassati, yathā yathā vā[^036-5] pana taṃ ñāteyyaṃ[^036-7] vā


<!-- PTS Page 037 -->
<a id="page-037"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 037]*

diṭṭheyyaṃ[^037-1] vā sacchikātayyaṃ[^037-2] vā, tathā[^037-3] tathā[^037-3] ñassati vā dakkhati vā[^037-4] sacchikarissati[^037-5] vā[^037-6] ti: ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. Etad ānuttariyaṃ Ānanda ñāṇānaṃ, yad[^037-6] idaṃ[^037-6] tattha tattha yathābhūtañāṇaṃ[^037-7]. Etasmā[^037-8] 'vāhaṃ[^037-9] Ānanda ñāṇā aññaṃ ñāṇaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā natthī ti vadāmi. Dasa yimāni Ānanda[^037-6] Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalāni, yehi balehi samannāgato Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti. Katamāni dasa?

3. Idh'; Ānanda[^037-10] Tathāgato ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Yam p'; Ānanda[^037-11] Tathāgato ṭhānañ ca ṭhānato aṭṭhānañ ca aṭṭhānato yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, idam p'; Ānanda Tathāgatassa Tathāgatabalaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma Tathāgato āsabhaṇṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati brahmacakkaṃ pavatteti.

##### *[PTS Page 039]*

XXIII.

1. Atthi bhikkhave dhammā kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya, atthi bhikkhave dhammā vācāya pahātabbā no kāyena, atthi bhikkhave dhammā neva kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā pahātabbā. Katame ca bhikkhave dhammā kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu akusalaṃ[^039-1] āpanno hoti kañci-d[^039-2]-eva desaṃ kāyena. Tam enaṃ anuvicca viññū sabrahmacārī evam āhaṃsu ‘āyasmā kho akusalaṃ āpanno kañci-d-eva desaṃ kāyena, sādhu vatāyasmā kāyaduccaritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvetū'; ti. So anuvicca viññūhi sabrahmacārīhi vuccamāno kāyaduccaritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāveti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave dhammā kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya. Katame ca bhikkhave dhammā vācāya pahātabbā no kāyena?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu akusalaṃ āpanno hoti kañci-d-eva desaṃ vācāya. Tam enaṃ anuvicca viññū sabrahmacārī evam āhaṃsu ‘āyasmā kho akusalaṃ āpanno kañci-d-eva desaṃ vācāya, sādhu vatāyasmā vacīduccaritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvetū'; ti. So anuvicca viññūhi sabrahmacārīhi vuccamāno vacīduccaritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāveti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave dhammā vācāya pahātabbā no kāyena. Katame ca bhikkhave dhammā neva kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā pahātabbā?

4. Lobho[^039-3] bhikkhave neva kāyena pahātabbo no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā pahātabbo. Doso bhikkhave . . . pe[^039-4] . . . Moho bhikkhave[^039-5] . . . Kodho bhikkhave[^039-5] . . . Upanāho bhikkhave[^039-5] . . . Makkho bhikkhave[^039-5] . . . Paḷāso


<!-- PTS Page 040 -->
<a id="page-040"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 040]*

bhikkhave[^040-1] . . . Macchariyaṃ bhikkhave neva kāyena pahātabbaṃ no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā pahātabbaṃ. Pāpikā bhikkhave issā neva kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā[^040-2] pahātabbā. Katamā ca[^040-3] bhikkhave pāpikā issā?

5. Idha bhikkhave ijjhati gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā dhanena vā dhaññena vā rajatena vā jātarūpena vā. Tatr'; aññatarassa dāsassa vā upavāsassa[^040-4] vā evaṃ hoti ‘aho vat'; imassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā na ijjheyya dhanena vā dhaññena vā rajatena vā jātarūpena vā'; ti. Samaṇo vā pana brāhmaṇo vā lābhī hoti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Tatr'; aññatarassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā evaṃ hoti ‘aho vata ayam[^040-5] āyasmā na lābhī assa cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan'; ti. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave pāpikā issā. Pāpikā[^040-6] bhikkhave issā neva kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā pahātabbā.

6. Pāpikā[^040-7] bhikkhave icchā neva kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā[^040-2] pahātabbā. Katamā ca bhikkhave[^040-8] pāpikā icchā?

7. Idha bhikkhave ekacco assaddho samāno ‘saddho ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, dussīlo samāno ‘sīlavā ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, appassuto[^040-9] samāno ‘bahussuto ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, saṅgaṇikārāmo samāno ‘pavivitto ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, kusīto samāno ‘āraddhaviriyo ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, muṭṭhassati samāno ‘upaṭṭhitasatī ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, asamāhito samāno ‘samāhito ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, duppañño samāno ‘paññavā ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati, akhīnāsavo[^040-10] samāno ‘khīṇāsavo ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti icchati.

Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave pāpikā icchā. Pāpikā[^041-1] bhikkhave icchā neva kāyena pahātabbā no vācāya, paññāya disvā disvā[^041-2] pahātabbā.

8. Tathā ayam[^041-7] āyasmā pajānāti, yathā pajānato doso na hoti . . . moho . . . kodho . . . upanāho . . . makkho . . . paḷāso . . . macchariyaṃ . . . pāpikā issā . . . pāpikā icchā na hoti; tathā h'; imaṃ āyasmantaṃ pāpikā icchā nābhibhuyya iriyatī ti.
