XXV.

1. Dasa yimāni[^046-1] bhikkhave kasiṇāyatanāni. Katamāni dasa?

2. Paṭhavīkasiṇam eko sañjānāti uddhaṃ adho tiriyaṃ[^046-2] advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ, āpokasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . .[^046-3] tejokasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . vāyokasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . nīlakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . pītakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . lohitakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . odātakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . ākāsakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . viññāṇakasiṇam eko sañjānāti uddhaṃ adho tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave dasa kasiṇāyatanānī ti. XXVI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Mahākaccāno Avantīsu[^046-4] viharati Kuraraghare[^046-5] pavatte pabbate. Atha kho Kālī upāsikā Kuraragharikā yenāyasmā Mahākaccāno ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho Kālī upāsikā Kuraragharikā āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Vuttam[^046-6] idaṃ bhante Bhagavatā Kumāripañhesu (Cf. S. I, p. 126): Atthassa pattiṃ[^046-7] hadayassa santiṃ[^046-8] jetvāna senaṃ piyasātarūpaṃ[^046-9] eko 'haṃ[^046-10] jhāyī[^046-11] sukham anubodhiṃ[^046-12], tasmā janena[^046-13] na[^046-14] karomi sakkhiṃ[^046-15] sakkhī[^046-16] na sampajjati kenaci me ti.


<!-- PTS Page 047 -->
<a id="page-047"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 047]*

Imassa nu[^047-1] kho bhante Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthārena attho[^047-2] daṭṭhabbo ti[^047-3]?

3. Paṭhavīkasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini eke[^047-4] samaṇabrāhmaṇā atthābhinibbattesuṃ[^047-5]. Yāvatā kho bhagini paṭhavīkasiṇasamāpattiparamatā tad abhiññāsi Bhagavā, tad abhiññāya[^047-6] Bhagavā ādim[^047-7] addasa ādīnavam addasa nissaraṇam addasa maggāmaggañāṇadassanam[^047-8] addasa. Tassa ādidassanahetu[^047-9] ādīnavadassanahetu nissaraṇadassanahetu maggāmaggañāṇadassanahetu atthassa patti hadayassa santi viditā hoti. Āpokasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini . . .[^047-10] tejokasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho[^047-11] bhagini . . . vāyokasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini . . . nīlakasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini . . . pītakasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini . . . lohitakasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini . . . odātakasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini . . . ākāsakasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini . . . viññāṇakasiṇasamāpattiparamā kho bhagini eke[^047-12] samaṇabrāhmaṇā atthābhinibbattesuṃ[^047-13]. Yāvatā kho bhagini viññāṇakasiṇasamāpattiparamatā tad abhiññāsi Bhagavā, tad abhiññāya[^047-6] Bhagavā ādim[^047-7] addasa ādīnavam addasa nissaraṇam addasa maggāmaggañāṇadassanam[^047-8] addasa. Tassa ādidassanahetu[^047-9] ādīnavadassanahetu nissaraṇadassanahetu maggāmaggañāṇadassanahetu atthassa patti hadayassa santi viditā hoti. Iti kho bhagini yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā Kumāripañhesu Atthassa pattiṃ[^047-14] hadayassa santiṃ[^047-15] jetvāna senaṃ piyasātarūpaṃ[^047-16] eko 'haṃ[^047-17] jhāyī[^047-18] sukham anubodhiṃ[^047-19],


<!-- PTS Page 048 -->
<a id="page-048"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 048]*

tasmā janena na[^048-1] karomi sakkhiṃ[^048-2] sakkhī[^048-3] na sampajjati kenaci me ti imassa kho bhagini Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena attho daṭṭhabbo ti[^048-4]. XXVII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Sāvatthiyaṃ[^048-5] piṇḍāya pāvisiṃsu[^048-6]. Atha kho tesaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ etad ahosi ‘atippago kho tāva Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ, yan nūna mayaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyāmā'[^048-7] ti. Atha kho te bhikkhū yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^048-8] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinne kho te bhikkhū te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā etad avocuṃ:--

2. Samaṇo āvuso Gotamo sāvakānaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘etha tumhe bhikkhave sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiññāya abhiññāya[^048-9] viharathā' ti. Mayam pi[^048-10] kho āvuso sāvakānaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ desema ‘etha tumhe āvuso sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiññāya abhiññāya[^048-11] viharathā'; ti. Idha no āvuso ko viseso ko adhippāyoso[^048-12] kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā[^048-13] Gotamassa amhākaṃ vā, yad idaṃ[^048-14]


<!-- PTS Page 049 -->
<a id="page-049"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 049]*

dhammadesanāya[^049-1] vā dhammadesanaṃ anusāsaniyā vā anusāsanin[^049-2] ti?

3. Atha kho te bhikkhū tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva abhinandiṃsu na ppaṭikkosiṃsu, anabhinanditvā appaṭikkositvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkamiṃsu[^049-3] ‘Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā'[^049-4] ti. Atha kho te bhikkhū Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkantā yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū Bhagavantaṃ etad avocuṃ:--

4. Idha mayaṃ bhante pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Sāvatthiyaṃ[^049-5] piṇḍāya pāvisimha[^049-6]. Tesaṃ no bhante amhākaṃ etad ahosi ‘atippago kho tāva Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ, yan nūna mayaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyāmā'; ti. Atha kho mayaṃ bhante yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkamimha, upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodimha, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^049-7] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdimha. Ekamantaṃ nisinne kho bhante aññatitthiyā paribbājakā amhe etad avocuṃ: Samaṇo āvuso Gotamo sāvakānaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘etha tumhe bhikkhave sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiññāya abhiññāya[^049-8] viharathā'; ti. Mayam pi kho āvuso sāvakānaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ desema ‘etha tumhe āvuso sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānātha, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiññāya abhiññāya[^049-9] viharathā'; ti. Idha no āvuso ko viseso ko adhippāyoso[^049-10] kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā Gotamassa[^049-11] amhākaṃ vā, yad idaṃ dhammadesanāya vā dhammadesanaṃ anusāsaniyā[^049-12] vā anusāsanin[^049-13] ti? Atha


<!-- PTS Page 050 -->
<a id="page-050"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 050]*

kho mayaṃ bhante tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva abhinandimha na ppaṭikkosimha, anabhinanditvā appaṭikkositvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkamimha[^050-1] ‘Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā'[^050-2] ti.

5. Evaṃ vādino bhikkhave aññatitthiyā paribbājakā evam assu vacanīyā: Eko āvuso pañho eko[^050-3] uddeso[^050-3] ekaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ, dve pañhā dve uddesā dve veyyākaraṇāni, tayo pañhā tayo uddesā tīṇi veyyākaraṇāni, cattāro pañhā cattaro uddesā cattari veyyākaraṇāni, pañca pañhā pañc'[^050-4] uddesā pañca veyyākaraṇāni, cha pañhā cha uddesā cha veyyākaraṇāni, satta pañhā satt'[^050-5] uddesā satta veyyākaraṇāni, aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭh'[^050-6] uddesā aṭṭha veyyākaraṇāni, nava pañhā nav'[^050-7] uddesā nava veyyākaraṇāni, dasa pañhā das'[^050-8] uddesā dasa veyyākaraṇānī ti? Evaṃ puṭṭhā bhikkhave aññatitthiyā paribbājakā na c'; eva[^050-9] sampāyissanti[^050-10] uttariñ[^050-11] ca[^050-11] vighātaṃ āpajjissanti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Yathā taṃ bhikkhave avisayasmiṃ. Nāhan taṃ bhikkhave passāmi sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya yo[^050-12] imesam pañhānaṃ veyyākaraṇena cittaṃ ārādheyya aññatra Tathāgatena vā Tathāgatasāvakena vā ito vā pana sutvā.

6. Eko pañho eko[^050-3] uddeso[^050-3] ekaṃ veyyākaraṇan ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ[^050-13]? Ekadhamme bhikkhave bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno[^050-14] sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca[^050-15] diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass' antakaro hoti. Katamasmiṃ ekadhamme? Sabbe sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā.


<!-- PTS Page 051 -->
<a id="page-051"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 051]*

Imasmiṃ kho bhikkhave ekadhamme bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno[^051-1] sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca[^051-2] diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Eko pañho eko[^051-3] uddeso[^051-3] ekaṃ veyyākaraṇan ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

7. Dve pañhā dve uddesā dve veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ[^051-4], kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Dvīsu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu dvīsu? Nāme ca rūpe ca. Imesu kho bhikkhave dvīsu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Dve pañhā dve uddesā dve veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

8. Tayo pañhā tayo uddesā tīṇi veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tīsu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass' antakaro hoti. Katamesu tīsu? Tīsu vedanāsu. Imesu kho bhikkhave tīsu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Tayo pañhā tayo uddesā tīṇi veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

9. Cattāro pañhā cattāro uddesā cattāri veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ[^051-4]?


<!-- PTS Page 052 -->
<a id="page-052"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 052]*

Catūsu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu catūsu? Catūsu āhāresu. Imesu kho bhikkhave catūsu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī samatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Cattāro pañhā cattāro uddesā cattāri veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

10. Pañca pañhā pañc'[^052-1] uddesā pañca veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Pañcasu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu pañcasu? Pañcasu upādānakkhandesu. Imesu kho bhikkhave pañcasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Pañca pañhā pañc'[^052-2] uddesā pañca veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

11. Cha pañhā cha uddesā cha veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Chasu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu chasu? Chasu ajjhattikesu[^052-3] āyatanesu. Imesu kho bhikkhave chasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 053 -->
<a id="page-053"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 053]*

Cha pañhā cha uddesā cha veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

12. Satta pañhā satt'[^053-1] uddesā satta veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Sattasu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu sattasu? Sattasu viññāṇaṭṭhitīsu. Imesu kho bhikkhave sattasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Satta pañhā satt'[^053-2] uddesā satta veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

13. Aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭh'; uddesā[^053-3] aṭṭha veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Aṭṭhasu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu aṭṭhasu? Aṭṭhasu lokadhammesu. Imesu kho bhikkhave aṭṭhasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭh'[^053-3] uddesā aṭṭha veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

14. Nava pañhā nav'[^053-4] uddesā nava veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Navasu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu navasu? Navasu sattāvāsesu.


<!-- PTS Page 054 -->
<a id="page-054"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 054]*

Imesu kho bhikkhave navasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Nava pañhā nav'[^054-1] uddesā nava veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

15. Dasa pañhā das'[^054-2] uddesā dasa veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Dasasu bhikkhave dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu dasasu? Dasasu akusalesu[^054-3] kammapathesu[^054-3]. Imesu kho bhikkhave dasasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Dasa pañhā das'[^054-2] uddesā dasa veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttan ti. XXVIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kajaṅgalāyaṃ[^054-4] viharati Veḷuvane. Atha kho sambahulā Kajaṅgalā[^054-5] upāsakā yena Kajaṅgalā[^054-6] bhikkhunī ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Kajaṅgalaṃ[^054-7] bhikkhuniṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho Kajaṅgalā[^054-5] upāsakā Kajaṅgalaṃ[^054-7] bhikkhuniṃ etad avocuṃ:--

2. Vuttam idaṃ ayye[^054-8] Bhagavatā Mahāpañhesu: eko pañho eko uddeso ekaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ, dve pañhā dve uddesā dve veyyākaraṇāni, tayo pañhā tayo uddesā tīṇi veyyākaraṇāni,


<!-- PTS Page 055 -->
<a id="page-055"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 055]*

cattāro pañhā cattāro uddesā cattāri veyyākaraṇāni, pañca pañhā pañc'[^055-1] uddesā pañca veyyākaraṇāni, cha pañhā cha uddesā cha veyyākaraṇāni, satta pañhā satt'[^055-2] uddesā satta veyyākaraṇāni, aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭh'[^055-3] uddesā aṭṭha veyyākaraṇāni, nava pañhā nav'[^055-4] uddesā nava veyyākaraṇāni, dasa pañhā das'[^055-5] uddesā dasa veyyākaraṇānī ti. Imassa nu kho ayye Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthārena attho daṭṭhabbo ti[^055-6]?

3. Na[^055-6] kho[^055-6] pan'; etaṃ[^055-6] āvuso Bhagavato[^055-7] sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahītaṃ, na pi manobhāvanīyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sammukhā sutaṃ samukhā paṭiggahītaṃ; api[^055-8] ca yathā[^055-9] m'; ettha khāyati[^055-10], taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ ayye'; ti kho Kajaṅgalā[^055-11] upāsakā Kajaṅgalāya[^055-12] bhikkhuniyā paccassosuṃ. Kajaṅgalā[^055-13] bhikkhunī etad avoca:--

4. Eko pañho eko[^055-14] uddeso ekaṃ veyyākaraṇan ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Ekadhamme āvuso bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamasmiṃ ekadhamme? Sabbe sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā. Imasmiṃ kho āvuso ekadhamme bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca ditth'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Eko[^055-15] pañho eko[^055-14] uddeso ekaṃ veyyākaraṇan ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 056 -->
<a id="page-056"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 056]*

5. Dve pañhā dve uddesā dve veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Dvīsu āvuso dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass' antakaro hoti. Katamesu dvīsu? Nāme ca rūpe ca . . . pe[^056-1] . . . Katamesu tīsu? Tīsu vedanāsu. Imesu kho āvuso tīsu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Tayo pañhā tayo uddesā tīṇi veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

6. Cattāro pañhā cattāro uddesā cattāri veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Catūsu āvuso dhammesu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacitto sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca ditth'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu catūsu? Catūsu satipaṭṭhānesu. Imesu kho āvuso catūsu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacitto sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Cattāro pañhā cattāro uddesā cattāri veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

7. Pañca pañhā pañc'[^056-2] uddesā pañca veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Pañcasu āvuso dhammesu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacitto sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu pañcasu? Pañcasu indriyesu . . . pe[^056-3] . . . Katamesu chasu?


<!-- PTS Page 057 -->
<a id="page-057"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 057]*

Chasu nissaraṇīyāsu dhātūsu . . .[^057-1] Katamesu sattasu? Sattasu bojjhaṅgesu . . .[^057-1] Katamesu aṭṭhasu? Ariye[^057-2] aṭṭhaṅgike[^057-3] magge[^057-4]. Imesu kho āvuso aṭṭhasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacitto sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Aṭṭha pañhā aṭṭh'[^057-5] uddesā aṭṭha veyyākaraṇāni ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

8. Nava pañhā nav'[^057-6] uddesā nava veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Navasu āvuso dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass' antakaro hoti. Katamesu navasu? Navasu sattāvāsesu. Imesu kho āvuso navasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā nibbindamāno sammā virajjamāno sammā vimuccamāno sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Nava[^057-7] pañhā nav'[^057-8] uddesā nava veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

9. Dasa pañhā das'[^057-9] uddesā dasa veyyākaraṇānī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Dasasu āvuso dhammesu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacitto sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Katamesu dasasu? Dasasu kusalesu[^057-10] kammapathesu[^057-10].


<!-- PTS Page 058 -->
<a id="page-058"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 058]*

Imesu kho āvuso dasasu dhammesu bhikkhu sammā subhāvitacitto sammā pariyantadassāvī sammatthābhisamecca diṭṭh'; eva dhamme dukkhass'; antakaro hoti. Dasa pañhā das'[^058-1] uddesā dasa veyyākaraṇānī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

10. Iti kho āvuso yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā Mahāpañhesu[^058-2]: Eko pañho eko[^058-3] uddeso ekaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ . . . pe[^058-4] . . . dasa veyyākaraṇānī ti[^058-5] imassa kho ahaṃ[^058-6] āvuso Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāmi[^058-7]. Ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhe āvuso Bhagavantaṃ yeva upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ paṭipuccheyyātha[^058-8]. Yathā no[^058-9] Bhagavā vyākaroti[^058-10], tathā naṃ dhāreyyāthā[^058-11] ti. ‘Evaṃ ayye'; ti kho Kajaṅgalā[^058-12] upāsakā[^058-13] Kajaṅgalāya[^058-14] bhikkhuniyā bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā Kajaṅgalaṃ bhikkhuniṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho Kajaṅgalā upāsakā, yāvatako ahosi Kajaṅgalāya bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ kathāsallāpo, taṃ sabbaṃ Bhagavato ārocesuṃ.

11. Sādhu sādhu gahapatayo. Paṇḍitā gahapatayo Kajaṅgalā bhikkhunī, mahāpaññā gahapatayo Kajaṅgalā bhikkhunī. Sace[^058-15] pi tumhe gahapatayo maṃ[^058-16] upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyātha[^058-17], aham pi c'; etaṃ[^058-18]


<!-- PTS Page 059 -->
<a id="page-059"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 059]*

evam eva[^059-1] vyākareyyaṃ[^059-2], yathā taṃ[^059-3] Kajaṅgalāya bhikkhuniyā vyākataṃ[^059-4]. Eso[^059-5] c'; eva[^059-6] tassa[^059-6] attho, evañ ca[^059-7] naṃ[^059-7] dhāreyyāthā[^059-8] ti. XXIX.

1. Yāvatā bhikkhave Kāsi-Kosalā, yāvatā rañño Pasenadissa[^059-9] Kosalassa vijitaṃ[^059-10], rājā tattha Pasenadi Kosalo[^059-11] aggam akkhāyati. Rañño pi[^059-12] kho bhikkhave Pasenadissa[^059-11] Kosalassa atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^059-13], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ nibbindanto[^059-14] agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

2. Yāvatā bhikkhave candimasuriyā pariharanti, disā bhanti virocamānā[^059-15], tāva sahassadhāloko, tasmiṃ sahassadhāloke sahassaṃ candānaṃ, sahassaṃ suriyānaṃ, sahassaṃ Sinerupabbatarājānaṃ, sahassaṃ Jambudīpānaṃ, sahassaṃ Aparagoyānānaṃ[^059-16], sahassaṃ Uttarakurūnaṃ, sahassaṃ Pubbavidehānaṃ[^059-17], cattāri mahāsamuddasahassāni[^059-18], cattāri mahārājasahassāni, sahassaṃ Cātummahārājikanaṃ[^059-19], sahassaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ, sahassaṃ Yāmānaṃ, sahassaṃ Tusitānaṃ[^059-20], sahassaṃ[^059-12] Nimmānaratīnaṃ[^059-12], sahassaṃ[^059-12] Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ[^059-21], sahassaṃ Brahmalokānaṃ; yāvatā bhikkhave sahassalokadhātu[^059-22], Mahābrahmā


<!-- PTS Page 060 -->
<a id="page-060"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 060]*

tattha aggam akkhāyati. Mahābrahmuno pi kho bhikkhave atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^060-1], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ nibbindanto[^060-2] agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

3. Hoti so bhikkhave samayo[^060-3], yaṃ ayaṃ loko saṃvaṭṭati, saṃvaṭṭamāne bhikkhave loke yebhuyyena sattā ābhassaravattanikā[^060-4] bhavanti. Te tattha honti manomayā pītibhakkhā[^060-5] sayampabhā antalikkhe carā subhaṭṭhāyino[^060-6] ciraṃ dīgham addhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti. Saṃvaṭṭamāne bhikkhave loke Ābhassarā devā aggam akkhāyanti[^060-7]. Ābhassarānaṃ pi kho bhikkhave devānaṃ atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^060-8], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ[^060-9] nibbindanto[^060-9] agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ[^060-10].

4. Dasa yimāni[^060-11] bhikkhave kasiṇāyatanāni. Katamāni dasa? Paṭhavīkasiṇam eko sañjānāti uddhaṃ adho tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ. Āpokasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . .[^060-12] Tejokasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . Vāyokasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . Nīlakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . Pītakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . Lohitakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . Odātakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . Ākāsakasiṇam eko sañjānāti . . . Viññāṇakasiṇam eko sañjānāti uddhaṃ adho tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave dasa kasiṇāyatanāni.

5. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave imesaṃ dasannaṃ kasiṇāyatanānaṃ, yad idaṃ viññāṇakasiṇam eko sañjānāti uddhaṃ adho tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ. Evaṃsaññino pi kho bhikkhave santi sattā. Evaṃsaññīnaṃ pi kho bhikkhave sattānaṃ atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^060-13], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 061 -->
<a id="page-061"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 061]*

passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ[^061-1] nibbindanto[^061-1] agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

6. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave abhibhāyatanāni. Katamāni aṭṭha? Ajjhattaṃ rūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni. Tāni[^061-2] abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Ajjhattaṃ rūpasaññī[^061-3] eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni[^061-2] abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ dutiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī[^061-4] eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ tatiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni. Seyyathā pi nāma ummāpupphaṃ[^061-5] nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ, seyyathā[^061-6] vā[^061-6] pana taṃ[^061-2] vatthaṃ bārāṇaseyyakaṃ ubhatobhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ: evam evaṃ[^061-7] ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ pañcamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni. Seyyathā pi nāma kaṇṇikārapupphaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ, seyyathā vā[^061-8] pana taṃ[^061-2] vatthaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 062 -->
<a id="page-062"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 062]*

bārāṇaseyyakaṃ ubhatobhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ: evam evaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati lohitakāni lohitakavaṇṇāni lohitakanidassanāni lohitakanibhāsāni. Seyyathā pi nāma bandhujīvakapupphaṃ[^062-1] lohitakaṃ lohitakavaṇṇaṃ lohitakanidassanaṃ lohitakanibhāsaṃ, seyyathā[^062-2] vā[^062-3] pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇaseyyakaṃ ubhatobhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ lohitakaṃ lohitakavaṇṇaṃ lohitakanidassanaṃ lohitakanibhāsaṃ: evam evaṃ[^062-4] ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati lohitakāni lohitakavaṇṇāni lohitakanidassanāni lohitakanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati odātāni odātavaṇṇāni odātanidassanāni odātanibhāsāni[^062-5]. Seyyathā pi nāma osadhitārakā odātā odātavaṇṇā odātanidassanā odātanibhāsā, seyyathā vā[^062-3] pana taṃ[^062-6] vatthaṃ bārāṇaseyyakaṃ ubhatobhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ odātaṃ odātavaṇṇaṃ odātanidassanaṃ odātanibhāsaṃ: evam evaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati odātāni odātavaṇṇāni odātanidassanāni odātanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni.

7. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave imesaṃ aṭṭhannaṃ abhibhāyatanānaṃ, yad idaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī[^062-7] eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati odātāni odātavaṇṇāni odātanidassanāni odātanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Evaṃsaññino[^062-8] pi kho bhikkhave santi sattā. Evaṃsaññīnaṃ pi kho bhikkhave sattānaṃ atth'; eva


<!-- PTS Page 063 -->
<a id="page-063"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 063]*

aññathattaṃ[^063-1], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ nibbindanto[^063-2] agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

8. Catasso imā bhikkhave paṭipadā. Katamā[^063-3] catasso? Dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiññā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiññā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiññā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiññā. Imā kho bhikkhave catasso paṭipadā.

9. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave imāsaṃ catunnaṃ paṭipadānaṃ, yad idaṃ sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiññā. Evaṃpaṭipannā pi kho bhikkhave santi sattā. Evaṃpaṭipannānaṃ pi kho[^063-4] bhikkhave sattānaṃ atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^063-5], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmim pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ nibbindanto[^063-6] agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

10. Catasso imā bhikkhave saññā. Katamā catasso? Parittam eko sañjānāti, mahaggatam eko sañjānāti, appamāṇam eko sañjānāti, ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanam eko sañjānāti. Imā kho bhikkhave catasso saññā.

11. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave imāsaṃ catunnaṃ saññānaṃ, yad idaṃ ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanam eko sañjānāti. Evaṃsaññino[^063-7] pi kho bhikkhave santi sattā. Evaṃsaññīnaṃ pi kho bhikkhave sattānaṃ atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^063-8], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ nibbindanto agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

12. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave bāhirakānaṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ, yad idaṃ ‘no c'; assaṃ[^063-9], no ca me siyā, na bhavissāmi, na me bhavissatī'; ti. Evaṃdiṭṭhino bhikkhave etaṃ[^063-10] pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^063-11]: yā cāyaṃ[^063-12] bhave appaṭikulyatā, sā[^063-13] c'; assa


<!-- PTS Page 064 -->
<a id="page-064"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 064]*

na bhavissati[^064-1], yā cāyaṃ[^064-2] bhavanirodhe pāṭikulyatā[^064-3], sā c'; assa na bhavissatī[^064-4] ti[^064-5]. Evaṃdiṭṭhino pi kho bhikkhave santi sattā. Evaṃdiṭṭhīnaṃ pi kho bhikkhave sattānaṃ atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^064-6], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ nibbindanto agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

13. Santi bhikkhave eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā . . . paramatthavisuddhiṃ[^064-7] paññāpenti[^064-8].

14. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave paramatthavisuddhiṃ[^064-7] paññāpentānaṃ[^064-8], yad idaṃ sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Te tad abhiññāya tassa sacchikiriyāya dhammaṃ desenti. Evaṃvādino pi kho bhikkhave santi sattā. Evaṃvadīnaṃ pi kho bhikkhave sattānaṃ atth'; eva aññathattaṃ[^064-9], atthi vipariṇāmo. Evaṃ passaṃ bhikkhave sutavā ariyasāvako tasmiṃ pi nibbindati, tasmiṃ nibbindanto[^064-10] agge virajjati, pageva hīnasmiṃ.

15. Santi bhikkhave eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā . . .[^064-11] paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ paññāpenti[^064-12].

16. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ paññāpentānaṃ[^064-12], yad idaṃ channaṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ ca aṭṭhaṅgamañ[^064-13] ca assādañ ca ādīnavañ ca nissaraṇañ ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādā-vimokho. Evaṃvādiṃ[^064-14] kho maṃ bhikkhave evamakkhāyiṃ[^064-15] eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā asatā tucchā musā abhūtena[^064-16] abbhācikkhanti ‘na[^064-17] samaṇo Gotamo kāmānaṃ pariññaṃ paññāpeti[^064-12], na rūpānaṃ pariññaṃ paññāpeti[^064-12], na vedanānaṃ pariññaṃ paññāpetī'[^064-12] ti[^064-18].


<!-- PTS Page 065 -->
<a id="page-065"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 065]*

17. Kāmānañ cāhaṃ[^065-1] bhikkhave pariññaṃ paññāpemi[^065-2], rūpānañ[^065-3] ca pariññaṃ paññāpemi[^065-2], ve danānañ ca pariññaṃ paññāpemi[^065-2], diṭṭh'; eva dhamme nicchāto nibbuto sītibhūto anupādā-parinibbānaṃ paññāpemī[^065-2] ti. XXX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tena kho pana samayena rājā Pasenadi[^065-4] Kosalo uyyodhikāya[^065-5] nivatto hoti vijitasaṅgāmo[^065-6] laddhādhippāyo. Atha kho rājā Pasenadi Kosalo yenārāmo tena pāyāsi. Yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānena[^065-7] gantvā[^065-7] yānā paccorohitvā pattiko 'va ārāmaṃ pāvisi.

2. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā bhikkhū abbhokāse caṅkamanti. Atha kho rājā Pasenadi Kosalo yena te bhikkhū ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā te bhikkhū etad avoca ‘kahaṃ[^065-8] nu kho bhante Bhagavā etarahi viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddho, dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ bhante taṃ[^065-9] Bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhan' ti. ‘Eso mahārāja vihāro[^065-10] saṃvutadvāro. Tena appasaddo upasaṅkamitvā ataramāno ālindaṃ[^065-11] pavisitvā ukkāsitvā aggaḷaṃ[^065-12] ākoṭehi. Vivarissati te Bhagavā dvāran'; ti.

3. Atha kho rājā Pasenadi[^065-13] Kosalo yena so vihāro saṃvutadvāro tena appasaddo upasaṅkamitvā ataramāno ālindaṃ[^065-11] pavisitvā ukkāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākotesi[^065-14]. Vivari Bhagavā dvāraṃ. Atha kho rājā Pasenadi Kosalo vihāraṃ[^065-15] pavisitvā Bhagavato pādesu sirasā nipatitvā Bhagavato pādāni mukhena ca paricumbati pāṇīhi ca parisambāhati nāmañ ca sāveti ‘rājāhaṃ bhante Pasenadi Kosalo, rājāhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 066 -->
<a id="page-066"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 066]*

bhante Pasenadi Kosalo'; ti. ‘Kam pana tvaṃ mahārāja atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^066-1] imasmiṃ sarīre evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ[^066-2] karosi, mettupahāraṃ[^066-3] upadaṃsesī'; ti?

4. Kataññutaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante kataveditaṃ sampassamāno Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi. Bhagavā hi[^066-4] bhante bahujanahitāya paṭipanno bahujanasukhāya bahuno[^066-5] janassa[^066-5] ariye[^066-6] ñāye[^066-7] patiṭṭhāpitā, yad idaṃ kalyāṇadhammatāya kusaladhammatāya. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā bahujanahitāya paṭipanno bahujanasukhāya bahuno[^066-8] janassa[^066-8] ariye[^066-6] ñāye[^066-9] patiṭṭhāpitā, yad idaṃ kalyāṇadhammatāya kusaladhammatāya: imam[^066-10] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^066-11] Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā sīlavā buddhasīlo ariyasīlo kusalasīlo[^066-12] kusalasīlena[^066-13] samannāgato[^066-13]. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā sīlavā buddhasīlo ariyasīlo kusalasīlo[^066-12] kusalasīlena[^066-13] samannāgato[^066-13]: imam[^066-14] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā dīgharattaṃ āraññako[^066-15] araññavanapatthāni[^066-16] pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevati. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā dīgharattaṃ āraññako[^066-17] arañña-


<!-- PTS Page 067 -->
<a id="page-067"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 067]*

vanapatthāni[^067-1] pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevati: imam[^067-2] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā santuṭṭho itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāre- na. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā santuṭṭho itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena: imam[^067-2] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā āhuneyyo pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā āhuneyyo pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa: imam[^067-3] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā, yāyaṃ kathā abhisallekhikā[^067-4] cetovivaraṇasappāyā, seyyathīdaṃ appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivekakathā asaṃsaggakathā viriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā paññākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, evarūpiyā[^067-5] kathāya nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā, yāyaṃ kathā abhisallekhikā[^067-4] cetovivaraṇasappāyā, seyyathīdaṃ appicchakathā . . . pe[^067-6] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, evarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī akasiralābhī: imam[^067-2] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^067-7] Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^067-8] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāma-


<!-- PTS Page 068 -->
<a id="page-068"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 068]*

lābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^068-1] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī: imam[^068-2] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^068-3] Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

11. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ‘ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo tisso pi jātiyo catasso pi jātiyo pañca pi jātiyo dasa pi jātiyo vīsam[^068-4] pi jātiyo tiṃsam pi jātiyo cattāḷīsam[^068-5] pi jātiyo paññāsam pi jātiyo jātisatam pi jātisahassam pi jātisatasahassam pi aneke pi saṃvaṭṭakappe aneke pi vivaṭṭakappe aneke pi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe amutrāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto amutra[^068-6] udapādiṃ[^068-6], tatrāpāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto idh'; upapanno'; ti: iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ[^068-7] anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^068-8] . . . iti sākāraṃ sauddesaṃ[^068-7] anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati: imam[^068-9] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^068-3] Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

12. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^068-10] satte passati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^068-11] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe duggate yathākammupage satte pajānāti ‘ime vata[^068-12] bhonto sattā kāyaduccaritena samannāgatā vacīduccaritena[^068-13] samannāgatā manoduccaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā


<!-- PTS Page 069 -->
<a id="page-069"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 069]*

micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapannā[^069-1]; ime vā pana bhonto sattā kāyasucaritena samannāgatā vacīsucaritena[^069-2] samannāgatā manosucaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapannā'[^069-1] ti: iti dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^069-3] satte passati[^069-4] cavamāne upapajjamāne[^069-5] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammupage satte pajānāti. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^069-3] . . .[^069-6] yathākammupage satte pajānāti: imam[^069-7] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^069-8] Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

13. Puna ca paraṃ bhante Bhagavā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Yam pi bhante Bhagavā āsavānaṃ khayā[^069-9] . . . pe[^069-10] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati: imam[^069-7] pi kho ahaṃ bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^069-11] Bhagavati evarūpaṃ paramanipaccākāraṃ karomi, mettupahāraṃ upadaṃsemi.

14. ‘Handa[^069-12] dāni mayaṃ bhante gacchāma bahukiccā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā'; ti. ‘Yassa dāni tvaṃ mahārāja kālaṃ maññasī'; ti. Atha kho rājā Pasenadi Kosalo uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmī ti. Mahāvaggo tatiyo. Tatr'[^069-13] uddānaṃ:


<!-- PTS Page 070 -->
<a id="page-070"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 070]*

Sīhādhimuttikāyena[^070-1] Cundena[^070-2] kasiṇena[^070-3] ca Kāli[^070-4] dve[^070-5] mahāpañhā[^070-6] Kosalehi[^070-7] pare[^070-8] duve[^070-9] ti. XXXI.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Upāli yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Upāli Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Kati nu kho bhante atthavase paṭicca Tathāgatassa sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ paññattaṃ, pātimokkhaṃ[^070-10] uddiṭṭhan ti? Dasa kho Upāli atthavase paṭicca Tathāgatena sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ paññattaṃ, pātimokkhaṃ uddiṭṭhaṃ. Katame dasa?

3. Saṅghasuṭṭhutāya saṅghaphāsutāya[^070-11] dummaṅkūnaṃ[^070-12] puggalānaṃ niggahāya pesalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya appasannānaṃ pasādāya pasannānaṃ bhīyobhāvāya saddhammaṭṭhitiyā vinayānuggahāya. Ime kho Upāli dasa atthavase paṭicca Tathāgatena sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ paññattaṃ pātimokkhaṃ uddiṭṭhan ti.

4. Kati nu kho bhante pātimokkhaṭṭhapanā[^070-13] ti[^070-14]? Dasa kho Upāli pātimokkhaṭṭhapanā[^070-15]. Katame dasa?

5. Pārājiko tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisinno hoti. Pārājikakathā vippakatā hoti. Anupasampanno tassaṃ parisāyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 071 -->
<a id="page-071"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 071]*

nisinno hoti. Anupasampannakathā vippakatā hoti. Sikkhaṃ[^071-1] paccakkhātako tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisinno hoti. Sikkhaṃ[^071-1] paccakkhātakathā vippakatā hoti. Paṇḍako tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisinno hoti. Paṇḍakakathā vippakatā hoti. Bhikkhunīdūsako tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisinno hoti. Bhikkhunīdūsakakathā vippakatā hoti. Ime kho Upāli dasa pātimokkhaṭṭhapanā[^071-2] ti. XXXII.

1. Katīhi nu kho bhante dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu ubbāhikāya[^071-3] sammannitabbo ti[^071-4]? Dasahi kho Upāli dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu ubbāhikāya sammannitabbo. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu[^071-5] vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^071-6] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^071-7] vacasā paricitā[^071-8] manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Ubhayāni[^071-9] kho pan'; assa pātimokkhāni vitthārena svāgatāni honti suvibhattāni suppavattīni[^071-10] suvinicchitāni suttaso anuvyañjanaso. Vinaye kho pana ṭhito hoti asaṃhiro[^071-11]. Paṭibalo hoti ubho atthapaccatthike[^071-12] saññāpetuṃ[^071-13] nijjhāpetuṃ[^071-14] pekkhetuṃ[^071-15] pasādetuṃ[^071-15]. Adhikaraṇasamuppāda-


<!-- PTS Page 072 -->
<a id="page-072"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 072]*

vūpasamakusalo[^072-1] hoti, adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇasamudayaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇanirodhaṃ[^072-2] jānāti[^072-2], adhikaraṇanirodhagāminiṃ[^072-3] paṭipadaṃ jānāti[^072-4]. Imehi kho Upāli dasahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu ubbāhikāya sammannitabbo ti. XXXIII.

1. Katīhi nu kho bhante dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā upasampādetabban ti[^072-5]? Dasahi kho Upāli dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā upasampādetabbaṃ[^072-6]. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu[^072-7] vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ[^072-8] savyañjanaṃ[^072-9] kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^072-10] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Pātimokkhaṃ kho pan'; assa[^072-11] vitthārena svāgataṃ[^072-12] hoti suvibhattaṃ suppavattaṃ suvinicchitaṃ suttaso anuvyañjanaso. Paṭibalo hoti gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpetuṃ vā. Paṭibalo hoti anabhiratiṃ vūpakāsetuṃ vā vūpakāsāpetuṃ vā. Paṭibalo hoti uppannaṃ kukkuccaṃ dhammato vinodetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti uppannaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammato vivecetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti adhisīle samādapetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti adhicitte samādapetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti adhipaññāya samādapetuṃ. Imehi kho Upāli dasahi dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā upasampādetabban ti[^072-13].


<!-- PTS Page 073 -->
<a id="page-073"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 073]*

XXXIV.

1. Katīhi nu kho bhante dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā nissayo[^073-1] dātabbo[^073-1] ti[^073-2]?  . . . pe[^073-3] . . . sāmaṇero[^073-4] upaṭṭhāpetabbo[^073-4] ti[^073-5]? Dasahi kho Upāli dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā sāmaṇero[^073-6] upaṭṭhāpetabbo[^073-7]. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhu sīlavā hoti . . . pe[^073-8] . . . samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Bahussuto[^073-9] hoti[^073-9] . . . pe . . .[^073-10] diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Pātimokkhaṃ kho pan'; assa vitthārena svāgataṃ hoti suvibhattaṃ suppavattaṃ suvinicchitaṃ suttaso anuvyañjanaso. Paṭibalo hoti gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpetuṃ vā. Paṭibalo hoti anabhiratiṃ vūpakāsetuṃ vā vūpakāsāpetuṃ vā. Paṭibalo hoti uppannaṃ kukkuccaṃ dhammato vinodetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti uppannaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammato vivecetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti adhisīle[^073-11] samādapetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti adhicitte samādapetuṃ. Paṭibalo hoti adhipaññāya samādapetuṃ. Imehi kho Upāli dasahi dhammehi samannāgatena bhikkhunā sāmaṇero[^073-12] upaṭṭhāpetabbo ti. XXXV.

1. ‘Saṅghabhedo saṅghabhedo'; ti bhante vuccati. Kittāvatā nu kho bhante saṅgho bhinno hotī ti?

2. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammo ti dīpenti, dhammaṃ[^073-13] adhammo[^073-13] ti[^073-13] dīpenti[^073-13], avinayaṃ[^073-14] vinayo


<!-- PTS Page 074 -->
<a id="page-074"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 074]*

ti dīpenti, vinayaṃ[^074-1] avinayo ti dīpenti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatena bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatena abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, appaññattaṃ Tathāgatena paññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, paññattaṃ Tathāgatena appaññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti. Te imehi dasahi vatthūhi avakassanti[^074-2], vavakassanti[^074-3], āvenikammāni[^074-4] karonti, āvenipātimokkhaṃ[^074-4] uddisanti[^074-5]. Ettāvatā kho Upāli saṅgho bhinno hotī ti. XXXVI.

1. ‘Saṅghasāmaggī saṅghasāmaggī'; ti bhante vuccati. Kittāvatā nu kho bhante saṅgho samaggo hotī ti?

2. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhū adhammaṃ adhammo ti dīpenti, dhammaṃ dhammo ti dīpenti, avinayaṃ avinayo ti dīpenti, vinayaṃ vinayo ti dīpenti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatena abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatena bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, appaññattaṃ Tathāgatena appaññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, paññattaṃ Tathāgatena paññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti. Te imehi dasahi vatthūhi na avakassanti, na vavakassanti[^074-6], na āvenikammāni[^074-4] karonti, na āvenipātimokkhaṃ[^074-4] uddisanti[^074-7]. Ettāvatā kho Upāli saṅgho samaggo hotī ti.


<!-- PTS Page 075 -->
<a id="page-075"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 075]*

1. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. ‘Saṅghabhedo saṅghabhedo'; ti bhante vuccati[^075-1]. Kittāvatā nu kho bhante saṅgho bhinno hotī ti?

3. Idh'; Ānanda bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammo ti dīpenti, dhammaṃ[^075-2] adhammo[^075-2] ti[^075-2] dīpenti[^075-2], avinayaṃ vinayo ti dīpenti[^075-3], vinayaṃ avinayo ti dīpenti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatena bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, bhāsitaṃ[^075-4] lapitaṃ Tathāgatena abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, anāciṇṇaṃ[^075-4] Tathāgatena āciṇṇaṃ[^075-5] Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, appaññattaṃ Tathāgatena paññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, paññattaṃ Tathāgatena appaññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti. Te imehi dasahi vatthūhi avakassanti, vavakassanti[^075-6], āvenikammāni[^075-7] karonti, āvenipātimokkhaṃ[^075-7] uddisanti[^075-8]. Ettāvatā kho Ānanda saṅgho bhinno hotī ti. XXXVIII.

1. Samaggaṃ pana bhante saṅghaṃ bhetvā[^075-9] kiṃ so pasavatī ti?

2. Kappaṭṭhiyaṃ[^075-10] Ānanda kibbisaṃ[^075-11] pasavatī ti.

3. Kiṃ pana bhante kappaṭṭhiyaṃ[^075-10] kibbisan[^075-11] ti?

4. Kappaṃ Ānanda nirayamhi paccatī ti.

Āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho saṅghabhedako vaggarato adhammaṭṭho yogakkhemato[^076-1] dhaṃsaṭi[^076-2] saṅghaṃ[^076-3] samaggaṃ bhetvāna[^076-4] kappaṃ nirayamhi paccatīti. XXXIX.

1. ‘Saṅghasāmaggī saṅghasāmaggī'; ti bhante vuccati[^076-5]. Kittāvatā nu kho bhante saṅgho samaggo hotī ti[^076-6]?

2. Idh'; Ānanda bhikkhū adhammaṃ adhammo ti dīpenti, dhammaṃ dhammo ti dīpenti, avinayaṃ avinayo ti dīpenti, vinayaṃ vinayo ti dīpenti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatena abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatena bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, appaññattaṃ Tathāgatena appaññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, paññattaṃ Tathāgatena paññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti. Te imehi dasahi vatthūhi na avakassanti, na vavakassanti[^076-7], na āvenikammāni[^076-8] karonti, na āvenipātimokkhaṃ[^076-8] uddisanti[^076-9]. Ettāvatā kho Ānanda saṅgho samaggo hotī ti. XL.

1. Bhinnaṃ pana bhante saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvā kiṃ so pasavatī ti?

2. Brahmaṃ Ānanda puññaṃ pasavatī ti[^076-10].

3. Kiṃ pana bhante brahmaṃ puññan ti?

4. Kappaṃ Ānanda saggamhi modatī ti.

Sukhā saṅghassa sāmaggī samaggānañ ca[^077-1] anuggaho samaggarato dhammaṭṭho yogakkhemā na dhaṃsati saṅghaṃ[^077-2] samaggaṃ katvāna kappaṃ saggamhi modatī ti. Upālivaggo[^077-3] catuttho. Tatr'[^077-4] uddānaṃ[^077-5]: Upāli[^077-6] ṭṭhapana[^077-6] ubbāho upasampadanissayena[^077-7] ca[^077-8] Sāmaṇero[^077-9] ca dve bhedā Ānandehi apare[^077-10] dve[^077-11] ti[^077-12]. XLI.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Upāli yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Upāli Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo, yena saṅghe bhaṇḍanakalahaviggahavivādā uppajjanti, bhikkhū ca[^077-13] na phāsu viharantī ti?

3. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammo ti dīpenti, dhammaṃ adhammo ti dīpenti, avinayaṃ vinayo ti dīpenti, vinayaṃ avinayo ti dīpenti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatena bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatena abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, appaññattaṃ Tathāgatena paññattaṃ Tathāgatenā

1. Kati nu kho bhante vivādamūlānī ti?

2. Dasa kho Upāli vivādamūlāni. Katamāni dasa?

3. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammo ti dīpenti, dhammaṃ adhammo ti dīpenti, avinayaṃ vinayo ti dīpenti, vinayaṃ avinayo ti dīpenti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatena bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ Tathāgatena abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, āciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatena anāciṇṇaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, appaññattaṃ Tathāgatena paññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti, paññattaṃ Tathāgatena appaññattaṃ Tathāgatenā ti dīpenti. Imāni kho Upāli dasa vivādamūlānī ti. XLIII.

1. Kati nu kho bhante vivādamūlānī ti?

2. Dasa kho Upāli vivādamūlāni. Katamāni dasa?

3. Idh'; Upāli bhikkhū anāpattiṃ[^078-3] āpattī ti dīpenti, āpattiṃ[^078-3] anāpattī ti dīpenti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ garukāpattī[^078-4] ti dīpenti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukāpattī ti dīpenti, duṭṭhullaṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhullāpattī ti dīpenti, aduṭṭhullaṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhullāpattī ti dīpenti, sāvasesaṃ āpattiṃ anavasesāpattī ti dīpenti, anavasesaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasesāpattī ti

##### *[PTS Page 079]*

dīpenti, sappaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ appaṭikammāpattī ti dīpenti, appaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ sappaṭikammāpattī ti dīpenti. Imāni kho Upāli dasa vivādamūlānī ti. XLIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kusinārāyaṃ viharati baliharaṇe[^079-1] vanasaṇḍe[^079-1]. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^079-2] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Codakena bhikkhave bhikkhunā paraṃ codetukāmena pañca dhamme ajjhattaṃ paccavekkhitvā pañca dhamme ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhapetvā[^079-3] paro codetabbo. Katame pañca dhammā ajjhattaṃ paccavekkhitabbā?

3. Codakena bhikkhave bhikkhunā paraṃ codetukāmena evaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ: parisuddhakāyasamācāro nu kho 'mhi, parisuddhen'; amhi kāyasamācārena samannāgato acchiddena appaṭimaṃsena, saṃvijjati nu kho me eso dhammo, udāhu no ti? No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu parisuddhakāyasamācāro hoti[^079-4] parisuddhena kāyasamācārena samannāgato acchiddena appaṭimaṃsena, tassa bhavanti vattāro: iṅgha tāva āyasmā kāyikaṃ sikkhassū ti. Iti 'ssa bhavanti vattāro.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave codakena bhikkhunā paraṃ codetukāmena evaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ: parisuddhavacīsamācāro nu kho 'mhi, parisuddhen'; amhi vacīsamācārena samannāgato acchiddena appaṭimaṃsena, saṃvijjati nu kho me[^079-5] eso[^079-5] dhammo, udāhu no ti? No ce[^079-6] bhikkhave bhikkhu parisuddhavacīsamācāro hoti parisuddhena vacīsamācārena samannāgato acchiddena appaṭimaṃsena, tassa bhavanti vattāro: iṅgha tāva āyasmā vācasikaṃ sikkhassū ti. Iti 'ssa bhavanti vattāro.


<!-- PTS Page 080 -->
<a id="page-080"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 080]*

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave codakena bhikkhunā paraṃ codetukāmena evaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ: mettaṃ nu kho me[^080-1] cittaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ[^080-2], saṃvijjati nu kho me eso dhammo, udāhu no ti? No ce bhikkhave bhikkhuno mettaṃ[^080-3] cittaṃ[^080-3] paccupaṭṭhitaṃ hoti[^080-4] sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ[^080-2], tassa bhavanti vattāro: iṅgha tāva āyasmā sabrahmacārīsu mettaṃ[^080-3] cittaṃ[^080-3] upaṭṭhapehī[^080-5] ti. Iti 'ssa bhavanti vattāro.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave codakena bhikkhunā paraṃ codetukāmena evaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ: bahussuto nu kho 'mhi sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpā[^080-6] me dhammā bahussutā honti[^080-7] dhatā[^080-8] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, saṃvijjati nu kho me eso dhammo, udāhu no ti? No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^080-9] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^080-10] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā[^080-11], tassa bhavanti vattāro: iṅgha tāva āyasmā āgamaṃ pariyāpuṇassū ti. Iti 'ssa bhavanti vattāro.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave codakena bhikkhunā paraṃ codetukāmena evaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ: ubhayāni nu[^080-12] kho[^080-13] me[^080-14] pātimokkhāni vitthārena svāgatāni[^080-15] honti[^080-4] suvibhattāni suppavattīni[^080-16] suvinicchitāni suttaso anuvyañjanaso, saṃvijjati nu kho me eso dhammo, udāhu no ti? No ce bhikkhave bhikkhuno ubhayāni pātimokkhāni

<a id="page-081"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 081]*

vitthārena svāgatāni[^081-1] honti suvibhattāni suppavattīni[^081-2] suvinicchitāni suttaso anuvyañjanaso, ‘idam panāyasmā kattha vuttaṃ Bhagavatā'; ti iti puṭṭho na sampāyati[^081-3], tassa bhavanti vattāro: iṅgha tāva āyasmā vinayaṃ sikkhassū ti[^081-4]. Iti 'ssa bhavanti vattāro. Ime pañca dhammā ajjhattaṃ paccavekkhitabbā.

8. Katame pañca dhammā ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpetabbā?

9. Kālena vakkhāmi no akālena, bhūtena vakkhāmi no abhūtena, saṇhena vakkhāmi no pharusena, atthasaṃhitena vakkhāmi no anatthasaṃhitena, mettacitto[^081-5] vakkhāmi no dosantaro[^081-6] ti[^081-7]. Ime pañca dhammā ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpetabbā[^081-8]. Codakena bhikkhave bhikkhunā paraṃ codetukāmena ime pañca dhamme ajjhattaṃ paccavekkhitvā ime pañca dhamme ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhapetvā[^081-9] paro codetabbo ti. XLV.

1. Dasa yime[^081-10] bhikkhave ādīnavā rājantepurappavesane. Katame dasa?

2. Idha bhikkhave rājā mahesiyā saddhiṃ nisinno hoti, tatra bhikkhu pavisati, mahesī vā[^081-11] bhikkhuṃ disvā sitaṃ pātukaroti, bhikkhu vā mahesiṃ disvā sitaṃ pātukaroti. Tattha rañño evaṃ hoti: addhā imesaṃ kataṃ vā karissanti vā ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rājā bahukicco bahukaraṇīyo aññataraṃ itthiṃ gantvā na[^081-12] sarati[^081-13]. Sā tena gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti. Tattha rañño evaṃ hoti: na kho idha


<!-- PTS Page 082 -->
<a id="page-082"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 082]*

añño koci pavisati aññatra pabbajitena, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño antepure aññataraṃ ratanaṃ nassati. Tattha rañño evaṃ hoti: na kho idha añño koci pavisati aññatra pabbajitena, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño antepure abbhantarā guyhamantā[^082-1] bahiddhā sambhedaṃ[^082-2] gacchanti. Tattha rañño evaṃ hoti: na kho idha añño koci pavisati aññatra pabbajitena, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño antepure pitā vā puttaṃ pattheti putto vā pitaraṃ pattheti. Tesaṃ evaṃ hoti: na kho idha añño koci pavisati aññatra pabbajitena, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcamo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rājā nīcaṭhāniyaṃ[^082-3] ucce ṭhāne ṭhapeti[^082-4]. Yesan taṃ amanāpaṃ, tesaṃ evaṃ hoti: rājā kho pabbajitena saṃsaṭṭho, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave chaṭṭho ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rājā uccaṭhāniyaṃ[^082-5] nīce[^082-6] ṭhāne ṭhapeti[^082-4]. Yesan taṃ amanāpaṃ, tesaṃ evaṃ hoti: rājā kho pabbajitena saṃsaṭṭho, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ kho bhikkhave sattamo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rājā akāle senaṃ uyyojeti. Yesan taṃ amanāpaṃ, tesaṃ evaṃ hoti: rājā kho pabbajitena saṃsaṭṭho, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rājā kāle senaṃ uyyojetvā antarāmaggato nivattāpeti[^082-7]. Yesan taṃ amanāpaṃ, tesaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 083 -->
<a id="page-083"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 083]*

evaṃ hoti: rājā kho pabbajitena saṃsaṭṭho, siyā nu kho pabbajitassa kamman ti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave navamo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane.

11. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave rañño antepuraṃ[^083-1] hatthisammadaṃ[^083-2] assasammadaṃ rathasammadaṃ, rajanīyāni[^083-3] rūpasaddagandharasaphoṭṭhabbāni, yāni na[^083-4] pabbajitasāruppāni[^083-5]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dasamo ādīnavo rājantepurappavesane. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa ādīnavā rājantepurappavesane ti. XLVI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ[^083-6] Nigrodhārāme. Atha kho sambahulā Sakkā[^083-7] upāsakā tadah'; uposathe yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinne kho Sakke upāsake Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Api nu kho[^083-8] tumhe Sakkā[^083-9] aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasathā ti? App ekadā mayaṃ[^083-4] bhante aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasāma[^083-10], app ekadā na upavasāmā ti[^083-11]. Tesaṃ vo[^083-12] Sakkā alābhā tesaṃ dulladdhaṃ, ye tumhe evaṃ sokasabhaye[^083-13] jīvite maraṇasabhaye[^083-14] jīvite app ekadā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasatha, app ekadā na upavasatha. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha Sakkā? Idha puriso yena kenaci[^083-15] kammaṭṭhānena anāpajja[^083-16] akusalaṃ divasaṃ aḍḍhakahāpaṇaṃ nibbiseyya,


<!-- PTS Page 084 -->
<a id="page-084"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 084]*

‘dakkho puriso uṭṭhānasampanno'; ti alaṃ vacanāyā[^084-1] ti? Evaṃ bhante. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha Sakkā? Idha puriso yena kenaci[^084-2] kammaṭṭhānena anāpajja akusalaṃ divasaṃ[^084-3] kahāpaṇaṃ nibbiseyya, ‘dakkho puriso uṭṭhānasampanno'; ti alaṃ vacanāyā ti?  Evaṃ bhante. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha Sakkā? Idha puriso yena kenaci[^084-2] kammaṭṭhānena anāpajja[^084-4] akusalaṃ divasaṃ dve kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . pe[^084-5] . . . tayo kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . cattāro kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . pañca kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . cha kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . satta kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . aṭṭha kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . nava kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . dasa kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . vīsa[^084-6] kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . tiṃsa[^084-7] kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . cattārisaṃ[^084-8] kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya . . . paññāsaṃ kahāpaṇe nibbiseyya[^084-9], ‘dakkho puriso uṭṭhānasampanno'; ti alaṃ vacanāyā ti? Evaṃ bhante. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha Sakkā? Api nu[^084-10] so puriso divase[^084-11] divase[^084-11] kahāpaṇasataṃ kahāpaṇasahassaṃ nibbisamāno laddhaṃ laddhaṃ nikkhipanto vassatāyuko[^084-12] vassasatajīvī mahantaṃ bhogakkhandhaṃ adhigaccheyyā ti? Evaṃ bhante. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha Sakkā? Api nu[^084-10] so puriso bhogahetu[^084-13] bhoganidānaṃ[^084-14] bhogādhikaraṇaṃ ekaṃ vā rattiṃ ekaṃ vā divasaṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā rattiṃ[^084-12] upaḍḍhaṃ[^084-12] vā[^084-12] divasaṃ ekantasukhapaṭisaṃvedī vihareyyā ti? No h'; etaṃ bhante. Taṃ kissa hetu? Kāmā hi bhante aniccā[^084-15] tucchā musā mosadhammā ti.

3. Idha kho[^084-5] pana vo Sakkā mama sāvako dasa vassāni appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamāno, satam pi vassāni satam pi vassasatāni


<!-- PTS Page 085 -->

##### *[PTS Page 085]*

satam pi vassasahassāni satam[^085-1] pi[^085-1] vassasatasahassāni[^085-1] ekantasukhapaṭisaṃvedī vihareyya. So ca khvassa[^085-2] sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sotāpanno. Tiṭṭhantu Sakkā dasa vassāni. Idha mama sāvako nava vassāni aṭṭha vassāni satta vassāni cha vassāni pañca vassāni cattāri vassāni tīṇi vassāni dve vassāni ekaṃ vassaṃ appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamāno, satam pi vassāni satam pi vassasatāni satam pi vassasahassāni satam[^085-1] pi[^085-1] vassasatasahassāni[^085-1] ekantasukhapaṭisaṃvedī[^085-3] vihareyya. So ca khvassa[^085-4] sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sotāpanno. Tiṭṭhatu Sakkā ekaṃ vassaṃ. Idha mama sāvako dasa māse appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamāno, satam pi vassāni satam[^085-5] pi[^085-5] vassasatāni[^085-5] satam pi vassasahassāni satam[^085-6] pi[^085-6] vassasatasahassāni[^085-6] ekantasukhapaṭisaṃvedī vihareyya. So ca khvassa[^085-7] sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sotāpanno. Tiṭṭhantu Sakkā dasa māsā. Idha mama sāvako nava māse aṭṭha māse satta māse cha māse pañca māse cattāro māse tayo māse dve māse ekaṃ[^085-8] māsaṃ aḍḍhamāsaṃ appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamāno, satam pi vassāni satam pi vassasatāni satam pi vassasahassāni satam[^085-1] pi[^085-1] vassasatasahassāni[^085-1] ekantasukhapaṭisaṃvedī[^085-3] vihareyya. So ca khvassa[^085-7] sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sotāpanno. Tiṭṭhatu Sakkā aḍḍhamāso. Idha mama sāvako dasa rattindive appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamāno satam pi vassāni satam pi vassasatāni satam pi vassasahassānī satam[^085-9] pi[^085-9] vassasatasahassāni[^085-9] ekantasukhapaṭisaṃvedī vihareyya. So ca khvassa[^085-7] sakadāhāmī vā anāgāmī[^085-5] vā[^085-5] apaṇṇakaṃ vā sotāpanno. Tiṭṭhantu Sakkā dasa rattindivā. Idha mama sāvako nava rattindive aṭṭha rattindive satta rattindive


<!-- PTS Page 086 -->
<a id="page-086"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 086]*

cha rattindive pañca rattindive cattāro rattindive tayo rattindive dve rattindive ekaṃ rattindivaṃ appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto yathā mayānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamāno, satam pi vassāni satam pi vassasatāni satam pi vassasahassāni satam[^086-1] pi[^086-1] vassasatasahassāni[^086-1] ekantasukhapaṭisaṃvedī vihareyya. So ca khvassa[^086-2] sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā apaṇṇakaṃ vā sotāpanno[^086-3]. Tesaṃ vo Sakkā alābhā tesaṃ dulladdhaṃ, ye tumhe evaṃ sokasabhaye[^086-4] jīvite maraṇasabhaye[^086-4] jīvite app ekadā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasatha, app ekadā na upavasathā ti Ete mayaṃ bhante ajja-t-agge aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasissāmā ti. XLVII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Atha kho Mahāli[^086-5] Licchavi[^086-6] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Mahāli[^086-5] Licchavi[^086-6] Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--

2. Ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya[^086-7] pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā ti? Lobho[^086-8] kho Mahāli hetu lobho paccayo pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, doso kho Mahāli hetu doso paccayo pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, moho kho Mahāli hetu moho paccayo pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, ayonisomanasikāro kho Mahāli hetu ayonisomanasikāro


<!-- PTS Page 087 -->
<a id="page-087"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 087]*

paccayo pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā, micchāpaṇihitaṃ kho Mahāli cittaṃ hetu micchāpaṇihitaṃ cittaṃ paccayo pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā. Ayaṃ kho Mahāli hetu ayaṃ paccayo pāpassa kammassa kiriyāya pāpassa kammassa pavattiyā ti[^087-1].

3. Ko pana bhante hetu ko paccayo kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā ti? Alobho kho Mahāli hetu alobho paccayo kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā, adoso kho Mahāli hetu[^087-2] adoso paccayo kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā, amoho kho Mahāli hetu amoho paccayo kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā, yonisomanasikāro kho Mahāli hetu yonisomanasikāro paccayo kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā, sammāpaṇihitaṃ kho Mahāli cittaṃ hetu sammāpaṇihitaṃ cittaṃ paccayo kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā[^087-3]. Ayaṃ kho Mahāli hetu ayaṃ paccayo kalyāṇassa kammassa kiriyāya kalyāṇassa kammassa pavattiyā. Ime ca[^087-4] Mahāli dasa dhammā loke na saṃvijjeyyuṃ, na yidha[^087-5] paññāyetha: adhammacariyā visamacariyā ti vā dhammacariyā samacariyā ti vā. Yasmā ca kho Mahāli ime dasa dhammā loke saṃvijjanti, tasmā paññāyati[^087-6] adhammacariyā visamacariyā ti[^087-7] vā dhammacariyā samacariyā ti[^087-7] vā[^087-7] ti[^087-8]. XLVIII.

1. Dasa yime bhikkhave dhammā pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbā. Katame dasa?

2. Vevaṇṇiyamhi ajjhūpagato ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Parapaṭibaddhā me jīvikā ti pabba-


<!-- PTS Page 088 -->
<a id="page-088"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 088]*

jitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Añño me ākappo karaṇīyo ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Kacci nu kho[^088-1] me attā[^088-2] sīlato na upavadatī[^088-3] ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ[^088-4] paccavekkhitabbaṃ[^088-4]. Kacci nu kho maṃ anuvicca viññū sabrahmacārī sīlato na upavadantī[^088-5] ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Sabbehi me piyehi manāpehi nānābhāvo vinābhāvo ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Kammassako 'mhi kammadāyādo kammayoni kammabandhu kammapaṭisaraṇo, yaṃ kammaṃ karissāmi kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, tassa dāyādo bhavissāmī ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Kathambhūtassa me rattindivā vītipatantī[^088-6] ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Kacci no kho 'haṃ[^088-7] suññāgāre abhiramāmī ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Atthi nu kho me uttarimanussadhammā[^088-8] alamariyañāṇadassanaviseso adhigato, so[^088-9] 'haṃ pacchime kāle sabrahmacārīhi puṭṭho na maṅku bhavissāmī ti pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa dhammā pabbajitena abhiṇhaṃ paccavekkhitabbā ti. XLIX.

1. Dasa yime[^088-10] bhikkhave dhammā sarīraṭṭhā. Katame dasa?

2. Sītaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā[^088-11] pipāsā uccāro passāvo kāyasaṃvaro. vacīsaṃvaro ājīvasaṃvaro ponobhaviko bhavasaṅkāro. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa dhammā sarīraṭṭhā ti. L.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tena kho pana samayena


<!-- PTS Page 089 -->
<a id="page-089"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 089]*

sambahulā bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkantā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ sannisinnā sannipatitā bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā[^089-1] vivādāpannā aññamaññaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā[^089-2] viharanti[^089-3]. Atha kho Bhagavā sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yen'[^089-4] upaṭṭhānasālā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Nisajja kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:--

2. Kāya nu'; ttha bhikkhave etarahi kathāya sannisinnā[^089-5], kā ca pana vo antarākathā vippakatā[^089-6] ti? Idha mayaṃ bhante pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkantā upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ sannisinnā sannipatitā bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpannā aññamaññaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharāmā ti. Na kho pan'; etaṃ[^089-7] bhikkhave tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhā[^089-8] agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ, yaṃ tumhe bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpannā aññamaññaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā vihareyyātha[^089-9]. Dasa yime[^089-10] bhikkhave dhammā sārāṇīyā[^089-11] piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya[^089-12] avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattanti. Katame dasa?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu[^089-13] vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu sīlavā hoti . . . pe[^089-14] . . . samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu: ayaṃ pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya[^089-15] avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^089-16] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^089-17] vacasā

##### *[PTS Page 090]*

paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu bahussuto hoti . . .[^090-1] diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo[^090-2] saṅgahāya[^090-2] avivādāya[^090-2] sāmaggiyā[^090-2] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu suvaco hoti sovacassakaraṇehi dhammehi samannāgato khamo padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu suvaco hoti sovacassakaraṇehi dhammehi samannāgato khamo padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo[^090-2] saṅgahāya[^090-2] avivādāya[^090-2] sāmaggiyā[^090-2] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uccāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni, tattha dakkho hoti analaso tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya samannāgato alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uccāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni tattha dakkho hoti analaso tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya samannāgato alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo[^090-2] saṅgahāya[^090-2] avivādāya[^090-2] sāmaggiyā[^090-2] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammakāmo hoti piyasamudāhāro abhidhamme abhivinaye uḷārapāmujjo. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammakāmo hoti piyasamudāhāro abhidhamme abhivinaye uḷārapāmujjo: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo[^090-2] saṅgahāya[^090-2] avivādāya[^090-2] sāmaggiyā[^090-2] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānam pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampādāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ

##### *[PTS Page 091]*

pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo[^091-1] saṅgahāya[^091-1] avivādāya[^091-1] sāmaggiyā[^091-1] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu santuṭṭho hoti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccaya bhesajjaparikkhārena. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu santuṭṭho hoti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccaya bhesajjaparikkhārena: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo[^091-2] piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo[^091-1] saṅgahāya[^091-1] avivādāya[^091-1] sāmaggiyā[^091-1] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu satimā hoti paramena satinepakkena samannāgato cirakatam pi cirabhāsitam pi saritā[^091-3] anussaritā[^091-3]: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo[^091-2] piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo[^091-1] saṅgahāya[^091-1] avivādāya[^091-1] sāmaggiyā[^091-1] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

12. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu paññavā hoti udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu paññavā hoti udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā: ayam pi dhammo sārāṇīyo[^091-2] piyakaraṇo garukaraṇo saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattati. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa dhammā sārāṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā ekībhāvāya saṃvattantī ti. Akkosavaggo[^091-4] pañcamo[^091-5]. Tatr'[^091-6] uddānaṃ:

##### *[PTS Page 092]*

Vivādā dve ca[^092-1] mūlāni Kusinārā pavesane Sakkā[^092-2] Mahāli dhammā[^092-3] ca sarīraṭṭhā ca[^092-4] bhaṇḍanā ti. Ānisaṃsapaṇṇāsako paṭhamo. LI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^092-5] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. No ce[^092-6] bhikkhave bhikkhu paracittapariyāyakusalo hoti, atha ‘sacittapariyāyakusalo[^092-7] bhavissāmī'[^092-8] ti. Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabbaṃ. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalo[^092-9] hoti?

3. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave itthi vā puriso vā daharo[^092-10] yuvā[^092-11] maṇḍanakajātiyo[^092-12] ādāse vā parisuddhe pariyodāte acche vā udapatte[^092-13] sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno, sace tattha[^092-14] passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ[^092-15] vā, tass' eva rajjassa[^092-14] vā[^092-14] aṅgaṇassa[^092-16] vā pahānāya vāyamati, no ce tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ[^092-17] vā, ten'; ev'; attamano hoti paripuṇṇasaṅkappo ‘lābhā vata me, parisuddhaṃ[^092-18] vata me'; ti: evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno[^092-19] paccavekkhanā[^092-20] bahukārā[^092-21] hoti[^092-22] kusalesu dhammesu: abhijjhālu


<!-- PTS Page 093 -->
<a id="page-093"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 093]*

nu[^093-1] kho bahulaṃ[^093-2] viharāmi, anabhijjhālu nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; vyāpannacitto[^093-3] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi[^093-4], avyāpannacitto[^093-5] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhito nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathīnamiddho nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; uddhato nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhato[^093-2] nu[^093-2] kho[^093-2] bahulaṃ[^093-2] viharāmi[^093-2]; vicikiccho[^093-6] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikiccho nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; kodhano nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, akkodhano[^093-7] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; saṃkiliṭṭhacitto nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacitto nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; sāraddhakāyo nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, asāraddhakāyo nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; kusīto nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhaviriyo nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; asamāhito nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhito nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmī ti.

4. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti: abhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi, vyāpannacitto[^093-3] bahulaṃ viharāmi, thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhito bahulaṃ viharāmi, uddhato bahulaṃ viharāmi, vicikiccho[^093-6] bahulaṃ viharāmi, kodhano bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃkiliṭṭhacitto bahulaṃ viharāmi, sāraddhakāyo bahulaṃ viharāmi, kusīto bahulaṃ viharāmi, asamāhito bahulaṃ viharāmī ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā tesaṃ[^093-8] yeva[^093-8] pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhi ca appaṭivāni ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave ādittacelo vā ādittasīso vā tass'; eva celassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ ca vāyāmañ ca ussāhañ ca ussoḷhiñ ca appaṭivāniñ ca satiñ ca sampajaññañ ca kareyya: evam eva kho bhikkhave tena[^093-9] bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhi ca appaṭivāni ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ.

##### *[PTS Page 094]*

5. Sace pana bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti: anabhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi, avyāpannacitto[^094-1] bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathīnamiddho bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhato bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikiccho bahulaṃ viharāmi, akkodhano[^094-2] bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacitto bahulaṃ viharāmi, asāraddhakāyo bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhaviriyo bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhito bahulaṃ viharāmī ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā tesu yeva kusalesu dhammesu patiṭṭhāya uttariṃ[^094-3] āsavānaṃ khayāya yogo karaṇīyo ti. LII.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Sāriputto bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Sāriputtassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Sāriputto etad avoca:--

2. No ce āvuso bhikkhu paracittapariyāyakusalo hoti, atha[^094-4] ‘sacittapariyāyakusalo[^094-5] bhavissāmī'[^094-6] ti. Evaṃ hi vo āvuso sikkhitabbaṃ. Kathañ cāvuso bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalo hoti?

3. Seyyathā pi āvuso itthi vā puriso vā daharo[^094-7] yuvā maṇḍanakajātiyo[^094-8] ādāse vā parisuddhe pariyodāte acche vā udapatte[^094-9] sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ paccavekkhamāno, sace tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ[^094-10] vā, tass'; eva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa[^094-11] vā pahānāya vāyamati, no ce tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ[^094-12] vā, ten'; ev'; attamano hoti paripuṇṇasaṅkappo ‘lābhā vata me, parisuddhaṃ vata me' ti: evam eva kho āvuso bhikkhuno[^094-13] paccavekkhanā[^094-14] bahukārā[^094-15] hoti kusalesu dhammesu: abhijjhālu nu kho


<!-- PTS Page 095 -->
<a id="page-095"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 095]*

bahulaṃ viharāmi, anabhijjhālu nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; vyāpannacitto[^095-1] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, avyāpannacitto[^095-1] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhito nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, vigatathīnamiddho nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; uddhato nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, anuddhato nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; vicikiccho[^095-2] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, tiṇṇavicikiccho nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; kodhano nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, akkodhano[^095-3] nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; saṃkiliṭṭhacitto nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, asaṃkiliṭṭhacitto nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; sāraddhakāyo nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; asāraddhakāyo nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; kusīto nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, āraddhaviriyo nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi; asamāhito nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmi, samāhito nu kho bahulaṃ viharāmī ti.

4. Sace āvuso bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti: abhijjhālu bahulam viharāmi . . . pe[^095-4] . . . asamāhito bahulaṃ viharāmī ti, tenāvuso bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhi ca appaṭivāni ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ. Seyyathā pi āvuso ādittacelo vā ādittasīso vā tass'; eva celassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ ca vāyāmañ ca ussāhañ ca ussoḷhiñ ca appaṭivāniñ ca satiñ ca sampajaññañ ca kareyya: evam eva kho āvuso tena bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhi ca appaṭivāni ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ.

5. Sace panāvuso bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti: anabhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi . . . pe[^095-4] . . . samāhito bahulaṃ viharāmī ti, tenāvuso bhikkhunā tesu yeva kusalesu dhammesu patiṭṭhāya uttariṃ[^095-5] āsavānaṃ khayāya yogo karaṇīyo ti.


<!-- PTS Page 096 -->
<a id="page-096"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 096]*

LIII.

1. Ṭhitim p'; ahaṃ[^096-1] bhikkhave na vaṇṇayāmi[^096-2] kusalesu dhammesu pageva pārihāniṃ. Vuddhiñ[^096-3] ca kho 'haṃ[^096-4] bhikkhave vaṇṇayāmi[^096-2] kusalesu dhammesu, no ṭhitiṃ no hāniṃ[^096-5]. Kathañ ca bhikkhave hāni hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no ṭhiti no vuddhi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu yattako hoti saddhāya sīlena sutena cāgena paññāya paṭibhānena[^096-6]. Tassa te dhammā neva tiṭṭhanti no vaḍḍhanti. Hānim etaṃ bhikkhave vadāmi kusalesu dhammesu, no ṭhitiṃ no vuddhiṃ. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave hāni hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no ṭhiti no vuddhi. Kathañ ca bhikkhave ṭhiti hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no hāni no vuddhi?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu[^096-7] yattako hoti saddhāya sīlena sutena cāgena paññāya paṭibhānena[^096-6]. Tassa te dhammā neva[^096-8] hāyanti no vaḍḍhanti. Ṭhitim etaṃ bhikkhave vadāmi kusalesu dhammesu, no hāniṃ no vuddhiṃ. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave ṭhiti hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no hāni no vuddhi. Kathañ ca bhikkhave vuddhi hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no ṭhiti no hāni?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu yattako hoti saddhāya sīlena sutena cāgena paññāya paṭibhānena[^096-6]. Tassa te dhammā neva tiṭṭhanti no hāyanti. Vuddhim etaṃ bhikkhave vadāmi kusalesu dhammesu, no ṭhitiṃ no hāniṃ. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave vuddhi hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no ṭhiti no hāni.

5. No ce bhikkhave bhikkhu paricittapariyāyakusalo hoti, atha[^096-9] ‘sacittapariyāyakusalo[^096-10] bhavissāmi'[^096-11] ti. Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabbaṃ. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu sacittapariyāyakusalo hoti?

7. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti: abhijjhālu bahulaṃ viharāmi, vyāpannacitto[^097-9] bahulaṃ viharāmi, thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhito bahulaṃ viharāmi, uddhato bahulaṃ viharāmi, vicikiccho[^097-10] bahulaṃ viharāmi, kodhano bahulaṃ viharāmi, saṃkiliṭṭhacitto bahulaṃ viharāmi, sāraddhakāyo bahulaṃ viharāmi, kusīto bahulaṃ viharāmi, asamāhito bahulaṃ viharāmī ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ
