30. Tayo bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo muṭṭhasaccaṃ pahātuṃ asampajaññaṃ pahātuṃ cetaso vikkhepaṃ pahātuṃ. Katame tayo?

31. Ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahāya ariyadhammaṃ asotukamyataṃ pahāya upārambhacittataṃ pahāya, ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhamme pahāya bhabbo muṭṭhasaccaṃ pahātuṃ asampajaññaṃ pahātuṃ cetaso vikkhepaṃ pahātuṃ.

32. Tayo bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammaṃ asotukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ. Katame tayo?

33. Uddhaccaṃ pahāya asaṃvaraṃ pahāya dussīlyaṃ pahāya, ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhamme pahāya bhabbo ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammaṃ asotukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ.

34. Tayo bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo uddhaccaṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ. Katame tayo?

35. Assaddhiyaṃ pahāya avadaññutaṃ pahāya kosajjaṃ pahāya, ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhamme pahāya bhabbo uddhaccaṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ.

36. Tayo bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadaññutaṃ pahātuṃ kosajjaṃ pahātuṃ. Katame tayo?

37. Anādariyaṃ pahāya dovacassataṃ pahāya pāpamittataṃ pahāya, ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhamme pahāya bhabbo assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadaññutaṃ pahātuṃ kosajjaṃ pahātuṃ.

38. Tayo bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dovacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ. Katame tayo?

39. Ahirikaṃ pahāya anottappaṃ pahāya pamādaṃ pahāya, ime kho bhikkhave tayo dhamme pahāya bhabbo anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dovacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ.

40. Hirimā 'yaṃ bhikkhave ottappī appamatto hoti. So appamatto samāno bhabbo anādariyaṃ pahātuṃ dovacassataṃ pahātuṃ pāpamittataṃ pahātuṃ. So kalyāṇamitto


<!-- PTS Page 149 -->
<a id="page-149"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 149]*

samāno bhabbo assaddhiyaṃ pahātuṃ avadaññutaṃ pahātuṃ kosajjaṃ pahātuṃ. So āraddhaviriyo samāno bhabbo uddhaccaṃ pahātuṃ asaṃvaraṃ pahātuṃ dussīlyaṃ pahātuṃ. So sīlavā samāno bhabbo ariyānaṃ adassanakamyataṃ pahātuṃ ariyadhammaṃ asotukamyataṃ pahātuṃ upārambhacittataṃ pahātuṃ. So anupārambhacitto samāno bhabbo muṭṭhasaccaṃ pahātuṃ asampajaññaṃ pahātuṃ cetaso vikkhepaṃ pahātuṃ. So avikkhittacitto samāno bhabbo ayonisomanasikāraṃ pahātuṃ kummaggasevanaṃ pahātuṃ cetaso līnattaṃ pahātuṃ. So alīnacitto samāno bhabbo sakkāyadiṭṭhiṃ pahātuṃ vicikicchaṃ pahātuṃ sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ pahātuṃ. So avicikiccho samāno bhabbo rāgaṃ pahātuṃ dosaṃ pahātuṃ mohaṃ pahātuṃ. So[^149-1] rāgaṃ pahāya dosaṃ pahāya mohaṃ pahāya bhabbo jātiṃ pahātuṃ[^149-2] jaraṃ pahātuṃ[^149-2] maraṇaṃ pahātun ti. LXXVII.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave asaddhammehi samannāgato kāko Katamehi dasahi?

2. Dhaṃsī ca pagabbho ca tintiṇo[^149-3] ca mahagghaso ca luddo[^149-4] ca akāruṇiko ca dubbalo ca oravitā[^149-5] ca muṭṭhassati ca necayiko[^149-6] ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi asaddhammehi samannāgato kāko.

3. Evam eva kho bhikkhave dasahi asaddhammehi samannāgato pāpabhikkhu. Katamehi dasahi?

4. Dhaṃsī ca pagabbho ca tintiṇo[^149-3] ca mahagghaso ca luddo[^149-4] ca akāruṇiko ca dubbalo ca oravitā[^149-7] ca muṭṭhassati ca necayiko[^149-6] ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi asaddhammehi samannāgato pāpabhikkhū ti.


<!-- PTS Page 150 -->
<a id="page-150"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 150]*

LXXVIII.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave asaddhammehi samannāgatā Nigaṇṭhā[^150-1]. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Assaddhā[^150-2] bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, dussīlā bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, ahirikā bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, anottappino bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, asappurisasambhattino[^150-3] bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, attukkaṃsakaparavambhakā[^150-4] bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsā[^150-5] ādhānagāhiduppaṭinissaggino[^150-6] bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, kuhakā[^150-7] bhikkhave[^150-7] Nigaṇṭhā[^150-7], pāpicchā bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā, micchādiṭṭhikā[^150-8] bhikkhave Nigaṇṭhā. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi asaddhammehi samannāgatā Nigaṇṭhā ti. LXXIX.

1. Dasa yimāni bhikkhave āghātavatthūni. Katamāni dasa?

2. ‘Anatthaṃ me acarī'; ti[^150-7] āghātaṃ[^150-7] bandhati[^150-7], ‘anatthaṃ me caratī'; ti[^150-7] āghātaṃ[^150-7] bandhati[^150-7], ‘anatthaṃ me carissatī'; ti āghātaṃ bandhati, ‘piyassa me manāpassa anatthaṃ acari . . . anatthaṃ carati . . . anatthaṃ carissatī'; ti āghātaṃ[^150-7] bandhati[^150-7], ‘appiyassa me amanāpassa atthaṃ acari . . . atthaṃ carati . . . atthaṃ carissatī'; ti āghātaṃ bandhati, aṭṭhāne ca kuppati. Imāni kho bhikkhave dasa āghātavatthūnī ti. LXXX.

1. Dasa yime bhikkhave āghātapaṭivinayā. Katame dasa?

2. ‘Anatthaṃ me acari, taṃ kut 'ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 151 -->
<a id="page-151"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 151]*

paṭivineti[^151-1], ‘anatthaṃ me carati, taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, ‘anatthaṃ me carissati, taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, ‘piyassa me manāpassa anatthaṃ acari . . . anatthaṃ[^151-2] carati . . . anatthaṃ[^151-2] carissati, taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, ‘appiyassa me amanāpassa atthaṃ acari . . . atthaṃ carati . . . atthaṃ carissati, taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, aṭṭhāne ca na kuppati. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa āghātapaṭivinayā ti. Ākaṅkhavaggo[^151-3] aṭṭhamo[^151-4]. Tatr'[^151-5] uddānaṃ: Ākaṅkho[^151-6] kaṇṭako iṭṭhā vaḍḍhī[^151-7] ca Migasālaya Abhabbo[^151-8] c'; eva[^151-8] kāko ca Nigaṇṭhā dve[^151-9] ca[^151-9] vatthunī[^151-10] ti[^151-11]. LXXXI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Campāyaṃ viharati Gaggarāya pokkharaṇiyā[^151-12] tīre. Atha kho āyasmā Bāhuno[^151-13] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Bāhuno Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘katīhi nu kho bhante dhammehi Tathāgato nissaṭo visaṃyutto vippamutto vimariyādikatena[^151-14] cetasā viharatī'; ti?


<!-- PTS Page 152 -->
<a id="page-152"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 152]*

2. Dasahi kho Bāhuna[^152-1] dhammehi Tathāgato nissaṭo visaṃyutto vippamutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharati. Katamehi dasahi?

3. Rūpena kho Bāhuna Tathāgato nissaṭo visaṃyutto vippamutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharati. Vedanāya kho Bāhuna . . .[^152-2] Saññāya kho Bāhuna . . . Saṅkhārehi kho Bāhuna . . . Viññāṇena kho Bāhuna . . . Jātiyā kho Bāhuna . . . Jarāya kho Bāhuna . . . Maraṇena kho Bāhuna . . . Dukkhehi kho Bāhuna . . . Kilesehi kho Bāhuna Tathāgato nissaṭo visaṃyutto vippamutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharati.

4. Seyyathā pi Bāhuna uppalaṃ vā padumaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ vā udake jātaṃ udake saṃvaḍḍhaṃ udakā accuggamma[^152-3] tiṭṭhati[^152-4] anupalittaṃ udakena, evam eva kho Bāhuna imehi dasahi dhammehi Tathāgato nissaṭo visaṃyutto vippamutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharatī ti. LXXXII.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno[^152-5] kho āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu assaddho[^152-6] samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ[^152-7] virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu dussīlo samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu appassuto samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat' Ānanda bhikkhu dubbacco[^152-8] samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye


<!-- PTS Page 153 -->
<a id="page-153"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 153]*

vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu pāpamitto samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu kusīto samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu muṭṭhassati samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu asantuṭṭho samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu pāpiccho samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat' Ānanda bhikkhu micchādiṭṭhiko samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu imehi[^153-1] dasahi[^153-2] dhammehi samannāgato imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati.

3. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu saddho samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu sīlavā samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu bahussuto sutadharo samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So[^153-3] vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu suvaco samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu upaṭṭhitasati samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 154 -->
<a id="page-154"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 154]*

āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu santuṭṭho samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat' Ānanda bhikkhu appiccho[^154-1] samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati[^154-2]. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiko samāno imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati. So vat'; Ānanda bhikkhu imehi[^154-3] dasahi dhammehi samannāgato imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. LXXXIII.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Puṇṇiyo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Puṇṇiyo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo yena app ekadā Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti, app ekadā[^154-4] na[^154-4] paṭibhātī'[^154-4] ti?

2. Saddho ca Puṇṇiya bhikkhu hoti, no ca upasaṅkamitā[^154-4], neva[^154-4] tāva[^154-5] Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti. Yato ca kho Puṇṇiya bhikkhu saddho ca hoti upasaṅkamitā ca, evaṃ Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti. Saddho ca Puṇṇiya bhikkhu hoti upasaṅkamitā ca, no ca payirupāsitā . . . pe[^154-6] . . . payirupāsitā ca, no ca paripucchitā . . . paripucchitā[^154-4] ca, no ca ohitasoto dhammaṃ suṇāti . . . ahitasoto[^154-7] ca[^154-4] dhammaṃ suṇāti, no ca sutvā dhammaṃ dhāreti . . . sutvā ca[^154-8] dhammaṃ dhāreti, no ca dhatānaṃ[^154-9] dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati . . . dhatānañ ca[^154-4] dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, no ca attham


<!-- PTS Page 155 -->
<a id="page-155"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 155]*

aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti . . . attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^155-1] hoti[^155-2], no[^155-2] ca[^155-2] kalyāṇavāco[^155-2] hoti[^155-2] kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya[^155-8] anelagaḷāya[^155-4] atthassa viññāpaniyā, kalyāṇavāco ca[^155-5] hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā, no ca sandassako hoti samādapako samuttejako sampahaṃsako sabrahmacārīnaṃ, neva[^155-6] tāva[^155-6] Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti.

3. Yato ca kho Puṇṇiya bhikkhu saddho ca hoti upasaṅkamitā ca payirupāsitā[^155-7] ca[^155-7] paripucchitā ca ohitasoto ca dhammaṃ suṇāti sutvā ca[^155-2] dhammaṃ dhāreti dhatānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^155-1] hoti kalyāṇavāco ca hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā sandassako ca hoti samādapako samuttejako sampahaṃsako sabrahmacārīnaṃ, evaṃ Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti. Imehi kho Puṇṇiya dasahi dhammehi samannāgatā ekantaṃ paṭibhānaṃ[^155-8] Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā hotī[^155-9] ti. LXXXIV.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Mahāmoggallānassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno etad avoca:--

2. Idh'; āvuso bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti[^155-10] ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathā-


<!-- PTS Page 156 -->
<a id="page-156"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 156]*

gatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo samanuyuñjati samanugāhati[^156-1] samanubhāsati. So Tathāgatena vā Tathāgatasāvakena vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalena paracittakusalena[^156-2] paracittapariyāyakusalena samanuyuñjiyamāno[^156-3] samanugāhiyamāno[^156-4] samanubhāsiyamāno[^156-4] iriṇaṃ[^156-5] āpajjati, vijinaṃ[^156-6] āpajjati, anayaṃ[^156-7] āpajjati[^156-7], vyasanaṃ āpajjati, anayavyasanaṃ āpajjati. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo[^156-8] paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca manasikaroti: Kin nu kho ayam āyasmā aññaṃ vyākaroti ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti? Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo[^156-8] paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti: Kodhano kho panāyam[^156-9] āyasmā kodhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, kodhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Upanāhī kho panāyam āyasmā upanāhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, upanāhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Makkhī kho panāyam āyasmā makkhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, makkhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Paḷāsī[^156-10] kho panāyam āyasmā paḷāsapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, paḷāsapariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Issukī kho panāyam āyasmā issāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, issāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Maccharī kho panāyam āyasmā maccherapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, maccherapariyuṭṭhānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 157 -->
<a id="page-157"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 157]*

kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Saṭho[^157-1] kho panāyam āyasmā sāṭheyyapariyuṭṭhitena[^157-2] cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, sāṭheyyapariyuṭṭhānaṃ[^157-2] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Māyāvī kho panāyam āyasmā māyāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, māyāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Pāpiccho kho panāyam āyasmā icchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, icchāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Muṭṭhassati[^157-3] kho panāyam āyasmā uttarikaraṇīye oramattakena visesādhigamena antarāvosānaṃ āpanno, antarāvosānagamanaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ.

3. So vatāvuso bhikkhu ime dasa dhamme appahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vatāvuso bhikkhu ime dasa dhamme pahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. LXXXV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Mahācundo Cetīsu viharati Sahajātiyaṃ[^157-4]. Tatra kho āyasmā Mahācundo bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo[^157-5] ti. Āvuso[^157-6] ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Mahācundassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Mahācundo etad avoca:--

2. Idhāvuso bhikkhu katthī hoti vikatthī[^157-7] adhigamesu ‘ahaṃ[^157-8] paṭhamaṃ[^157-9] jhānaṃ[^157-9] samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 158 -->
<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 158]*

ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pī'; ti. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo[^158-1] paracittapariyāyakusalo samanuyuñjati samanugāhati samanubhāsati. So Tathāgatena vā Tathāgatasāvakena vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalena paracittakusalena paracittapariyāyakusalena samanuyuñjiyamāno samanugāhiyamāno samanubhāsiyamāno iriṇaṃ[^158-2] āpajjati, vijinaṃ[^158-3] āpajjati, anayaṃ āpajjati, vyasanaṃ āpajjati, anayavyasanaṃ[^158-4] āpajjati[^158-4]. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo[^158-5] paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca manasikaroti: Kin nu kho ayam āyasmā katthī hoti[^158-1] vikatthī adhigamesu[^158-6] ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi . . . pe[^158-7] . . . ahaṃ saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pī'; ti[^158-1]? Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti: Dīgharattaṃ kho[^158-8] ayam āyasmā khaṇḍakārī chiddakārī sabalakārī kammāsakārī na santatakārī[^158-9] na santatavuttī[^158-9] sīlesu. Dussīlo ayam āyasmā, dussīlyaṃ[^158-10] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Assaddho[^158-11] kho panāyam āyasmā[^158-12], assaddhiyaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Appassuto kho pana ayam āyasmā anācāro, appasaccaṃ[^158-13] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam


<!-- PTS Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 159]*

etaṃ. Dubbaco kho panāyam āyasmā[^159-1], dovacassatā[^159-2] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Pāpamitto kho panāyam āyasmā, pāpamittatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Kusīto kho panāyam āyasmā, kosajjaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Muṭṭhassati[^159-3] kho panāyam āyasmā, muṭṭhasaccaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Kuhako kho panāyam āyasmā, kuhanā[^159-4] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Dubbharo[^159-5] kho panāyam āyasmā, dubbharatā[^159-6] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Duppañño kho panāyam āyasmā, duppaññatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ.

3. Seyyathā[^159-7] pi āvuso sahāyako[^159-8] sahāyakaṃ evaṃ vadeyya ‘yadā te samma dhanena[^159-9] dhanakaraṇīyaṃ assa[^159-10], yācissasi[^159-11] maṃ dhanaṃ, dassāmi te dhanan'; ti. So kismiñci[^159-12]-d-eva dhanakaraṇīye samuppanne sahāyako sahāyakaṃ evaṃ vadeyya ‘attho me samma dhanena, dehi me dhanan'; ti. So evaṃ vadeyya ‘tena hi samma idha khaṇāhī'[^159-13] ti. So tatra khaṇanto nādhigaccheyya. So[^159-14] evaṃ vadeyya ‘alikaṃ maṃ samma avaca, tucchakaṃ maṃ samma avaca: idha khaṇāhī'; ti. So evaṃ vadeyya ‘nāhan taṃ samma alikaṃ avacaṃ, tucchakaṃ avacaṃ, tena hi samma[^159-15] idha khaṇāhī'; ti. So tatra pi khaṇanto nādhigaccheyya. So evaṃ vadeyya ‘alikaṃ maṃ samma avaca, tucchakaṃ maṃ samma[^159-16] avaca: idha khaṇāhī'; ti. So evaṃ vadeyya ‘nāhan taṃ samma alikaṃ avacaṃ, tucchakaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 160]*

avacaṃ, tena hi samma[^160-1] idha khaṇāhī'; ti. So[^160-2] tatra pi khaṇanto nādhigaccheyya. So evaṃ vadeyya ‘alikaṃ maṃ samma avaca, tucchakaṃ maṃ samma avaca: idha khaṇāhī'; ti. So evaṃ vadeyya ‘nāhan taṃ samma alikaṃ avacaṃ, tucchakaṃ avacaṃ, api ca aham eva ummādaṃ pāpuṇiṃ cetaso vipariyāyan'; ti. Evam eva kho āvuso bhikkhu katthī hoti vikatthī adhigamesu ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pi, ahaṃ saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pī'[^160-3] ti[^160-3]. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo samanuyuñjati samanugāhati samanubhāsati. So Tathāgatena vā Tathāgatasāvakena vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalena paracittakusalena[^160-3] paracittapariyāyakusalena samanuyuñjiyamāno samanugāhiyamāno samanubhāsiyamāno iriṇaṃ[^160-4] āpajjati, vijinaṃ[^160-5] āpajjati, anayaṃ āpajjati, vyasanaṃ āpajjati, anayavyasanaṃ āpajjati. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca manasikaroti: Kin nu kho ayam āyasmā katthī hoti[^160-3] vikatthī[^160-3] adhigamesu ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi[^160-6] pi[^160-6] . . . pe[^160-7] . . . ahaṃ saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ samāpajjāmi pi vuṭṭhahāmi pī'; ti[^160-8]? Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti:


<!-- PTS Page 161 -->

##### *[PTS Page 161]*

Dīgharattaṃ kho ayam āyasmā khaṇḍakārī chiddakārī sabalakārī sammāsakārī na santatakārī[^161-1] na santatavuttī[^161-2] sīlesu. Dussīlo ayam āyasmā, dussīlyaṃ[^161-3] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Assaddho[^161-4] kho panāyam āyasmā, assaddhiyaṃ[^161-4] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Appassuto kho panāyam āyasmā anācāro, appasaccaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Dubbaco kho panāyam āyasmā, dovacassatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Pāpamitto kho panāyam āyasmā, pāpamittatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Kusīto kho panāyam āyasmā, kosajjaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Muṭṭhassati[^161-5] kho panāyam āyasmā, muṭṭhasaccaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Kuhako kho panāyam āyasmā, kuhanā[^161-6] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Dubbharo[^161-7] kho pana ayam āyasmā, dubbharatā[^161-7] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Duppañño kho pana ayam āyasmā, duppaññatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ.

4. So vatāvuso bhikkhu ime dasa dhamme appahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vatāvuso bhikkhu ime dasa dhamme pahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. LXXXVI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Mahākassapo Rājagahe viharati Veḷuvane Kalandakanivāpe. Tatra kho āyasmā


<!-- PTS Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 162]*

Mahākassapo bhikkhū āmantesi[^162-1]:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo[^162-2] ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Mahākassapassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Mahākassapo etad avoca:--

2. Idhāvuso bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti[^162-3] ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo samanuyuñjati samanugāhati samanubhāsati. So Tathāgatena vā Tathāgatasāvakena vā jhāyinā samāpattikusalena paracittakusalena paracittapariyāyakusalena samanuyuñjiyamāno samanugāhiyamāno samanubhāsiyamāno iriṇaṃ[^162-4] āpajjati, vijinaṃ[^162-5] āpajjati, anayaṃ āpajjati, vyasanaṃ[^162-6] āpajjati[^162-6], anayavyasanaṃ āpajjati. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca manasikaroti: Kin nu kho ayam āyasmā aññaṃ vyākaroti ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti? Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti: Adhimāniko[^162-7] kho[^162-8] ayam āyasmā adhimānasacco[^162-9] appatte pattasaññī akate katasaññī anadhigate adhigatasaññī adhimānena aññaṃ vyākaroti ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo[^162-10] paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca manasikaroti: Kin nu kho ayam āyasmā nissāya adhimāniko adhimānasacco appatte pattasaññī akate katasaññī anadhigate adhigatasaññī adhimānena aññaṃ vyākaroti ‘khīṇā


<!-- PTS Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 163]*

jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti? Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti: ‘Bahussuto kho panāyam[^163-1] āyasmā sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa[^163-2] dhammā bahussutā dhatā[^163-3] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā[^163-4] diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, tasmā ayam āyasmā adhimāniko adhimānasacco appatte pattasaññī akate katasaññī anadhigate adhigatasaññī adhimānena aññaṃ vyākaroti ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti. Tam enaṃ Tathāgato vā Tathāgatasāvako vā jhāyī samāpattikusalo paracittakusalo[^163-5] paracittapariyāyakusalo evaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti: Abhijjhālu kho panāyam āyasmā abhijjhāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, abhijjhāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Vyāpādo[^163-6] kho panāyam āyasmā vyāpādapariyuṭṭhitena[^163-7] cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, vyāpādapariyuṭṭhānaṃ[^163-7] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Thīnamiddho[^163-8] kho panāyam āyasmā thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Uddhato kho panāyam āyasmā uddhaccapariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, uddhaccapariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Vicikiccho[^163-9] kho panāyam āyasmā vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitena cetasā bahulaṃ viharati, vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Kammārāmo kho panāyam āyasmā kammarato kammārāmataṃ anuyutto,


<!-- PTS Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 164]*

kammārāmatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Bhassārāmo kho panāyam āyasmā bhassarato bhassārāmataṃ anuyutto, bhassārāmatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Niddārāmo kho panāyam āyasmā niddārato niddārāmataṃ anuyutto, niddārāmatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Saṅgaṇikārāmo kho panāyam āyasmā saṅgaṇikārato saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyutto, saṅgaṇikārāmatā kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ. Muṭṭhassati[^164-1] kho panāyam āyasmā uttarikaraṇīye[^164-2] oramattakena visesādhigamena antarāvosānaṃ āpanno, antarāvosānagamanaṃ[^164-3] kho pana Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye parihānam etaṃ.

3. So vatāvuso bhikkhu ime dasa dhamme appahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vatāvuso bhikkhu ime dasa dhamme pahāya imasmiṃ dhammavinaye vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjissatī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjatī ti. LXXXVII

1. Tatra kho Bhagavā Kālakaṃ[^164-4] bhikkhuṃ ārabbha[^164-5] bhikkhū[^164-5] āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^164-6] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu adhikaraṇiko hoti adhikaraṇasamathassa na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu adhikaraṇiko hoti adhikaraṇasamathassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya[^164-7] na garuttāya[^164-8] na bhāvanāya na sāmaññāya[^164-9] na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.


<!-- PTS Page 165 -->
<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 165]*

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu na sikkhākāmo hoti[^165-1] sikkhāsamādānassa[^165-2] na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu na sikkhākāmo hoti sikkhāsamādānassa[^165-3] na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya[^165-4] na garuttāya na bhāvanāya na sāmaññāya na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu pāpiccho hoti icchāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu pāpiccho hoti icchāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya . . . pe[^165-5] . . . na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu kodhano hoti kodhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu kodhano hoti kodhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya . . . pe[^165-5] . . . na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu makkhī hoti makkhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu makkhī hoti makkhavinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya . . . pe[^165-5] . . . na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu saṭho[^165-6] hoti sāṭheyyavinayassa[^165-7] na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu saṭho[^165-8] hoti sāṭheyyavinayassa[^165-7] na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya . . . pe[^165-5] . . . na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu māyāvī hoti māyāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu māyāvī hoti māyāvinayassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya . . . pe[^165-5] . . . na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.


<!-- PTS Page 166 -->
<a id="page-166"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 166]*

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammānaṃ na nisāmakajātiyo[^166-1] hoti dhammanisantiyā na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammānaṃ na nisāmakajātiyo[^166-2] hoti dhammanisantiyā na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya . . . pe[^166-3] . . . na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

10. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu na paṭisallāno hoti paṭisallānassa na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu na paṭisallāno hoti paṭisallānassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya . . . pe[^166-3] . . . ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

11. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ na paṭisanthārako[^166-4] hoti paṭisanthārakassa na vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ na paṭisanthārako hoti paṭisanthārakassa na vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo na piyattāya na garuttāya na bhāvanāya na sāmaññāya na ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

12. Evarūpassa bhikkhave bhikkhuno kiñcāpi evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata maṃ sabrahmacārī sakkareyyuṃ garukareyyuṃ māneyyuṃ pūjeyyun'; ti. Atha kho naṃ[^166-5] sabrahmacārī na c'; eva[^166-6] sakkaronti na[^166-7] garukaronti na[^166-7] mānenti na[^166-7] pūjenti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi 'ssa[^166-8] bhikkhave viññū sabrahmacārī te pāpake akusale dhamme appahīne samanupassanti.

13. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave assakhaluṅkassa[^166-9] kiñcāpi evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata maṃ manussā ājānīyaṭṭhāne[^166-10] ṭhapeyyuṃ ājānīyabhojanañ ca bhojeyyuṃ ājānīyaparimajjanañ ca parimajjeyyun'; ti. Atha kho naṃ manussā na c'; eva ājānīyaṭṭhāne ṭhapenti na ca ājānīyabhojanaṃ bhojenti na ca ājānīyaparimajjanaṃ parimajjanti[^166-11].


<!-- PTS Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 167]*

Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi 'ssa bhikkhave viññū manussā tāni sāṭheyyāni[^167-1] kūṭeyyāni[^167-2] jimheyyāni[^167-3] vaṅkeyyāni[^167-4] appahīnāni samanupassanti. Evam eva kho bhikkhave evarūpassa bhikkhuno kiñcāpi evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata maṃ sabrahmacārī sakkareyyuṃ garukareyyuṃ māneyyuṃ pūjeyyun'; ti. Atha kho naṃ sabrahmacārī na c'; eva[^167-5] sakkaronti na[^167-6] garukaronti na[^167-6] mānenti na[^167-6] pūjenti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi 'ssa bhikkhave viññū sabrahmacārī te pāpake akusale dhamme appahīne samanupassanti.

14. Idha pana[^167-7] bhikkhave bhikkhu na[^167-8] adhikaraṇiko[^167-8] hoti adhikaraṇasamathassa vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu na[^167-9] adhikaraṇiko[^167-9] hoti adhikaraṇasamathassa vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

15. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu sikkhākāmo hoti sikkhāsamādānassa[^167-10] vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu sikkhākāmo hoti sikkhāsamādānassa[^167-10] vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo piyattāya . . .[^167-11] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

16. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu appiccho hoti icchāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu appiccho hoti icchāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo . . .[^167-12] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

17. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu akkodhano[^167-13] hoti kodhavinayassa vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu akkodhano[^167-13] hoti kodhavinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo . . . pe[^167-14] . . . ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

18. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu amakkhī hoti makkhavinayassa[^167-15] vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu amakkhī hoti makkhavinayassa[^167-15] vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo . . .[^167-16] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

##### *[PTS Page 168]*

19. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu asaṭho[^168-1] hoti sāṭheyyavinayassa[^168-2] vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu asaṭho[^168-1] hoti sāṭheyyavinayassa[^168-2] vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo . . .[^168-3] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

20. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu amāyāvī hoti māyāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu amāyāvī hoti māyāvinayassa vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo . . .[^168-3] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

21. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammānam nisāmakajātiyo[^168-4] hoti dhammanisantiyā vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu dhammānaṃ nisāmakajātiyo[^168-4] hoti dhammanisantiyā vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo . . .[^168-3] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

22. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisallāno hoti paṭisallānassa vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu paṭisallāno hoti paṭisallānassa vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo . . .[^168-3] ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

23. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ paṭisanthārako[^168-5] hoti paṭisanthārakassa vaṇṇavādī. Yam pi bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ paṭisanthārako[^168-5] hoti paṭisanthārakassa vaṇṇavādī, ayam pi dhammo piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmaññāya ekībhāvāya saṃvattati.

24. Evarūpassa bhikkhave bhikkhuno kiñcāpi na evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata maṃ sabrahmacārī sakkareyyuṃ garukareyyuṃ māneyyum pūjeyyun'; ti. Atha kho naṃ sabrahmacārī sakkaronti garukaronti mānenti pūjenti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi 'ssa bhikkhave viññū sabrahmacārī te pāpake akusale dhamme pahīne samanupassanti.

25. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave bhaddassa assājānīyassa kiñcāpi na evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata naṃ manussā ājānīyaṭṭhāne[^168-6] ṭhapeyyuṃ[^168-7] ājānīyabhojanañ ca bhojjeyyuṃ ājānīyaparimajjanañ ca parimajjeyyun'; ti. Atha kho naṃ manussā ājānīyaṭṭhāne[^168-8] ṭhapenti ājānīyabhojanañ ca


<!-- PTS Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 169]*

bhojenti ājānīyaparimajjanañ ca parimajjanti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi 'ssa bhikkhave viññū manussā tāni sāṭheyyāni[^169-1] kūṭeyyāni[^169-2] jimheyyāni vaṅkeyyāni[^169-3] pahīnāni samanupassanti. Evam eva kho bhikkhave evarūpassa bhikkhuno kiñcāpi na[^169-4] evaṃ icchā uppajjeyya ‘aho vata maṃ sabrahmacārī sakkareyyuṃ garukareyyuṃ māneyyuṃ pūjeyyun'; ti. Atha kho naṃ sabrahmacārī sakkaronti garukaronti mānenti pūjenti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi 'ssa bhikkhave viññū sabrahmacārī te pāpake akusale dhamme pahīne samanupassantī ti. LXXXVIII.

1. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu akkosakaparibhāsako[^169-5] ariyupavādī sabrahmacārīnaṃ, aṭṭhānam etaṃ anavakāso[^169-6], yaṃ so[^169-7] dasannaṃ vyasanānaṃ aññataraṃ[^169-8] vyasanaṃ na[^169-9] nigaccheyya. Katamesaṃ dasannaṃ?

2. Anadhigataṃ nādhigacchati[^169-10], adhigatā[^169-11] parihāyati[^169-12], saddhammassa na vodāyati[^169-13], saddhammesu vā adhimāniko hoti, anabhirato[^169-14] vā brahmacariyaṃ carati, aññataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ[^169-15] āpajjati, gāḷhaṃ vā rogātaṅkaṃ phusati, ummādaṃ vā pāpuṇāti cittakkhepaṃ, sammūḷho kālaṃ karoti, kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjati[^169-16]. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu akkosakaparibhāsako ariyupavādī sabrahmacārīnaṃ, aṭṭhānam etaṃ anavakāso[^169-6], yaṃ so[^169-7] imesaṃ dasannaṃ vyasanānaṃ aññataraṃ[^169-8] vyasanaṃ na[^169-9] nigaccheyyā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 170]*

LXXXIX.

1. Atha kho Kokāliko[^170-1] bhikkhu yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Kokāliko bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘pāpicchā bhante Sāriputta-Moggallānā pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā'; ti. ‘Mā h'; evaṃ Kokālika, mā[^170-2] h'; evaṃ[^170-2] Kokālika[^170-2], pasādehi Kokālika Sāriputta-Moggallānesu cittaṃ, pesalā SāriputtaMoggallānā'; ti. Dutiyam pi kho Kokāliko bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kiñcāpi me bhante Bhagavā saddhāyiko paccayiko, atha kho pāpicchā'; va[^170-3] Sāriputta-Moggallānā pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā'; ti. ‘Mā h'; evaṃ Kokālika, mā h'; evaṃ Kokālika, pasādehi Kokālika Sāriputta-Moggallānesu cittaṃ, pesalā Sāriputta-Moggallānā'; ti. Tatiyam pi kho Kokāliko bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ . . . pe[^170-4] . . . pesalā Sāriputta-Moggallānā'; ti.

2. Atha kho Kokāliko bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi. Acirapakkantassa ca Kokālikassa bhikkhuno sāsapamattīhi[^170-5] piḷakāhi[^170-6] sabbo kāyo phuṭo[^170-7] ahosi. Sāsapamattiyo hutvā muggamattiyo ahesuṃ, muggamattiyo hutvā kalāyamattiyo[^170-8] ahesuṃ. Kalāyamattiyo hutvā kolaṭṭhimattiyo ahesuṃ. Kolaṭṭhimattiyo hutvā kolamattiyo ahesuṃ. Kolamattiyo hutvā āmalakamattiyo[^170-9] ahesuṃ. Āmalakamattiyo[^170-9] hutvā beluvasalāṭukamattiyo[^170-10] ahesuṃ. Beluvasalāṭukamattiyo[^170-10] hutvā billamattiyo[^170-11] ahesuṃ. Billamattiyo hutvā pabhijjiṃsu.


<!-- PTS Page 171 -->
<a id="page-171"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 171]*

Pubbañ ca lohitañ ca pagghariṃsu[^171-1]. Svāssudaṃ[^171-2] kadalipattesu[^171-3] seti[^171-4] maccho va visagilito[^171-5].

3. Atha kho Tuduppaccekabrahmā[^171-6] yena Kokāliko bhikkhu ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā vehāse ṭhatvā Kokālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ etad avoca ‘pasādehi Kokālika Sāriputta-Moggallānesu cittaṃ, pesalā Sāriputta-Moggallānā'; ti. ‘Ko 'si tvaṃ āvuso'; ti? ‘Ahaṃ Tuduppaccekabrahmā'; ti. ‘Nanu tvaṃ āvuso Bhagavatā anāgāmī vyākato[^171-7], atha kiñcarahi[^171-8] idhāgato, passa[^171-9] yāva[^171-10] te idaṃ aparaddhan'; ti. Atha kho Tuduppaccekabrahmā Kokālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi: Purisassa hi jātassa kuthāri[^171-11] jāyate mukhe yāya chindati[^171-12] attānaṃ bālo dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ. Yo nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati taṃ vā nindati yo pasaṃsiyo vicināti mukhena so kaliṃ[^171-13] kalinā[^171-14] tena sukhaṃ na vindati. Appamatto[^171-15] ayaṃ kali yo akkhesu dhanaparājayo sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā ayam eva mahattaro[^171-16] kali yo sugatesu[^171-17] manaṃ padosaye[^171-18]. Sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ chattiṃsati[^171-19] pañca[^171-20] ca[^171-20] abbudāni[^171-21] yam ariyagarahī[^171-22] nirayaṃ upeti vācaṃ manañ[^171-23] ca[^171-23] paṇidhāya[^171-24] pāpakan ti.


<!-- PTS Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 172]*

4. Atha kho Kokāliko bhikkhu ten'; evābādhena kālam akāsi. Kālakato ca Kokāliko bhikkhu padumanirayaṃ[^172-1] upapajjati[^172-2] Sāriputta-Moggallānesu cittaṃ āghātetvā[^172-3]. Atha kho Brahmā Sahampati abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo[^172-4] kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhito kho Brahmā Sahampati Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘Kokāliko bhante bhikkhu kālakato, kālakato ca bhante Kokāliko bhikkhu padumanirayaṃ[^172-1] upapanno[^172-5] Sāriputta-Moggallānesu cittaṃ āghātetvā'[^172-3] ti. Idam avoca Brahmā Sahampati, idaṃ vatvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyi.

5. Atha kho Bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi: Imaṃ bhikkhave rattiṃ Brahmā Sahampati abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo[^172-6] kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhito kho bhikkhave Brahmā Sahampati maṃ etad avoca ‘Kokāliko bhante bhikkhu kālakato, kālakato ca bhante Kokāliko bhikkhu padumanirayaṃ upapanno[^172-7] SāriputtaMoggallānesu cittaṃ āghātetvā'[^172-8] ti. Idam avoca bhikkhave Brahmā Sahampati, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyī ti.

6. Evam vutte aññataro bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kīva[^172-9] dīghaṃ[^172-10] nu kho bhante padumaniraye[^172-11] āyuppamāṇan'; ti? ‘Dīghaṃ kho bhikkhu padumaniraye[^172-11] āyuppamāṇaṃ, taṃ[^172-12] na[^172-13] sukaraṃ saṃkhātuṃ "ettakāni


<!-- PTS Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 173]*

vassānī" ti iti[^173-1] vā "ettakāni vassasatānī" ti iti[^173-1] vā "ettakāni vassasahassānī" ti iti[^173-1] vā "ettakāni vassasatasahassānī" ti iti[^173-1] vā'; ti. ‘Sakkā pana bhante upamā kātun'; ti? ‘Sakkā bhikkhū'; ti. Bhagavā avoca:-- Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsatikhāriko Kosalako tilavāho, tato[^173-2] puriso vassasatassa[^173-3] vassasatassa[^173-3] accayena[^173-4] ekam ekaṃ tilaṃ uddhareyya. Khippataraṃ kho so bhikkhu vīsatikhāriko Kosalako tilavāho[^173-5] iminā upakkamena parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gaccheyya, na[^173-6] tveva[^173-6] eko abbudo nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati abbudā nirayā[^173-7], evam eko[^173-8] nirabbudo nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati nirabbudā nirayā, evam eko ababo[^173-9] nirayo[^173-10]. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati ababā[^173-11] nirayā, evam eko ahaho[^173-12] nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati ahahā nirayā, evam eko aṭaṭo nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati aṭaṭā nirayā, evam eko kumudo nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati kumudā nirayā, evam eko sogandhiko nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati sogandhikā nirayā, evam eko uppalako[^173-13] nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu uppalakā[^173-14] nirayā, evam eko puṇḍarīko nirayo. Seyyathā pi bhikkhu vīsati puṇḍarīkā nirayā, evam eko padumo nirayo. Padumaṃ kho pana bhikkhu nirayaṃ Kokāliko bhikkhu upapanno[^173-15] Sāriputta-Moggallānesu cittaṃ āghātetvā ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna[^173-16] Sugato athāparaṃ etad avoca Satthā:


<!-- PTS Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 174]*

Purisassa hi jātassa kuthāri[^174-1] jāyate mukhe yāya chindati attānaṃ bālo dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ. Yo nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati taṃ vā nindati yo pasaṃsiyo vicināti mukhena so kaliṃ kalinā[^174-2] tena[^174-2] sukhaṃ na[^174-3] vindati. Appamatto[^174-4] ayaṃ kali yo akkhesu dhanaparājayo sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā ayam eva mahattaro[^174-5] kali yo sugatesu manaṃ padosaye[^174-6]. Sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ chattiṃsati[^174-7] pañca ca[^174-8] abbudāni yam ariyagarahī[^174-9] nirayaṃ upeti[^174-10] vācaṃ manañ ca paṇidhāya[^174-11] pāpakan ti. XC.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca: Kati nu kho Sāriputta khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balāni, yehi balehi samannāgato khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti? Dasa bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balāni, yehi balehi samannāgato khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti. Katamāni dasa?

2. Idha bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aniccato sabbe saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti.


<!-- PTS Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 175]*

Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aniccato sabbe saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aṅgārakāsupamā kāmā[^175-1] yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aṅgārakāsupamā kāmā[^175-2] yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno vivekaninnaṃ cittaṃ hoti vivekapoṇaṃ[^175-3] vivekapabbhāraṃ vivekaṭṭhaṃ[^175-4] nekkhammābhirataṃ[^175-5] vyantibhūtaṃ[^175-6] sabbaso āsavaṭṭhāniyehi dhammehi. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno vivekaninnaṃ cittaṃ hoti vivekapoṇaṃ[^175-3] vivekapabbhāraṃ vivekaṭṭhaṃ nekkhammābhirataṃ[^175-5] vyantibhūtaṃ[^175-6] sabbaso āsavaṭṭhāniyehi dhammehi, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno cattāro sammappadhānā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā . . . pe[^175-7] . . . cattāro iddhippādā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā . . .[^175-8] pañc' indriyāni bhāvitāni[^175-9] honti[^175-9] subhāvitāni[^175-9] . . . pañca balāni


<!-- PTS Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 176]*

bhāvitāni honti subhāvitāni . . . satta bojjhaṅgā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā . . . ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo bhāvito hoti subhāvito. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo bhāvito hoti subhāvito, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti. Imāni kho bhante dasa khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balāni, yehi balehi samannāgato khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti ‘khīṇā me āsavā'; ti. Theravaggo[^176-1] navamo[^176-2]. Tatr'[^176-3] uddānaṃ: Bāhuno[^176-4] c'; Ānando ca[^176-5] Puṇṇiyo ca[^176-5] vyākaraṇaṃ[^176-6] Katthī[^176-7] aññādhikaraṇaṃ[^176-8] Kokāliko ca balāni cā[^176-9] ti. XCI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 177]*

2. Dasa yime gahapati kāmabhogī[^177-1] santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmiṃ. Katame dasa?

3. Idha gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena, adhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena na attānaṃ sukheti pīneti[^177-2], na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti.

4. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena, adhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti.

5. Idha[^177-3] pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena, adhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti.

6. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena pi asāhasena pi, dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena pi asāhasena pi na attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti.

7. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena pi asāhasena pi, dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena pi asāhasena pi attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti.

8. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena pi asāhasena pi, dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena pi asāhasena pi attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti.

9. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena na attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti.

10. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā


<!-- PTS Page 178 -->
<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 178]*

asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti.

11. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti, te ca bhoge gadhito[^178-1] mucchito[^178-2] ajjhāpanno anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañño paribhuñjati.

12. Idha pana gahapati ekacco kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti, te[^178-3] ca bhoge agadhito[^178-1] amucchito anajjhāpanno ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañño paribhuñjati.

13. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^178-4] kāmabhogī adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena, adhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena na attānaṃ sukheti pīneti. na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī tīhi[^178-5] ṭhānehi gārayho. Adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena gārayho, na attānam sukheti pīnetī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena gārayho, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karotī ti iminā tatiyena ṭhānena gārayho. Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī imehi tīhi ṭhānehi gārayho.

14. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^178-6] kāmabhogī adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena, adhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī dvīhi ṭhānehi gārayho, ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso. Adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena gārayho, attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī ti iminā ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karotī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena gārayho. Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī imehi dvīhi ṭhānehi gārayho, iminā ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso.


<!-- PTS Page 179 -->
<a id="page-179"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 179]*

15. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^179-1] kāmabhogī adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena, adhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī ekena ṭhānena gārayho, dvīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso. Adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasenā ti iminā ekena ṭhānena gārayho, attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, saṃvibhajati puññāni karotī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso. Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī iminā ekena ṭhānena gārayho, imehi dvīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso.

16. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^179-1] kāmabhogī dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena pi asāhasena pi, dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena pi asāhasena[^179-2] pi[^179-2] na attānaṃ sukheti pīneti[^179-3], na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, tīhi ṭhānehi gārayho. Dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasenā ti iminā ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena gārayho, na attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī[^179-3] ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena gārayho, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karotī ti iminā tatiyena ṭhānena gārayho. Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī iminā ekena[^179-4] ṭhānena pāsaṃso, imehi tīhi ṭhānehi gārayho.

17. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^179-1] kāmabhogī dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena pi asāhasena pi, dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena pi asāhasena pi attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī dvīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso[^179-5], dvīhi[^179-5] ṭhānehi[^179-5] gārayho. Dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena gārayho[^179-6], attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karotī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena gārayho.


<!-- PTS Page 180 -->
<a id="page-180"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 180]*

Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī imehi dvīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso, imehi dvīhi ṭhānehi gārayho.

18. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^180-1] kāmabhogī dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasena pi asāhasena pi, dhammādhammena bhoge pariyesitvā sāhasena pi asāhasena pi attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī tīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso, ekena ṭhānena gārayho. Dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, adhammena bhoge pariyesati sāhasenā ti iminā ekena ṭhānena gārayho, attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, saṃvibhajati puññāni karotī ti iminā tatiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso. Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī imehi tīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso, iminā[^180-2] ekena[^180-2] ṭhānena[^180-2] gārayho[^180-2].

19. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^180-1] kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena na[^180-3] attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, dvīhi ṭhānehi gārayho. Dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasenā ti iminā ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, na attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī[^180-4] ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena gārayho, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karotī ti iminā dutiyena thānena gārayho Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī iminā ekena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, imehi dvīhi ṭhānehi gārayho.

20. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^180-1] kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karoti: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī dvīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso, ekena ṭhānena gārayho. Dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, na saṃvibhajati na puññāni karotī ti iminā ekena ṭhānena gārayho.
